# The engine

This document describes the engine as this branch holds it (the branch one-universe, 2026-10-23, the law of one Node on the owner's decision, ALGEBRA.md, The one Node: one act list for every world, the loader's vocabulary content and run parameters alone with every former switch of the acts refused as an unknown key (section 4), alpha and the trace's weight carried inside the Node's one division, the volume form on every board, the tension lines stepped as the shear's momentum at the volume's wall, the click one act, section 10's item 18; the branch atom-arranged, 2026-10-19: the engine of main at 3.0.0 with the family's sign under exchange, section 4's key `exchange`, act 6's factor and section 10's item 9, the three declared words entered on the owner's word of 2026-10-19, and the click that runs back on the owner's word of 2026-10-19, section 4's key `reach` (the key `meeting` of that day refused by name since 2026-10-20), act 6's readings in the time's order, section 10's item 11 and the module `meeting_back.py`, and the write at the birth with the births' book and the lay across parts on the owner's word of 2026-10-20, act 6, section 10's items 11 and 12; at the merge the sentence reads again as main holds it), the engine V2 at 3.0.0 (the click as one act, the spin and the atom on the V2 step, 2026-10-19): Rule3 only, written once in the law's flux form (`core/rule3.rule3`: w (a_next - 2 a_now + a_before) + r' - r = SUM over the six Links of R_ij (m_ij - a_now) - D_i a_now, the step `flux.py` gathering the Links' sum Link by Link; the owner's word of 2026-10-16, the engine matches the law in its implementation and not only in its results; the results those of 2.0.0 on the unclicked worlds and the paper's run, `tools/step_oracle.py`, the clicked worlds' readings moved where the law's dated lines say (CHANGELOG.md, 3.0.0), and a level above the amplitude bound 6,949 refused), every act on a level Rule3's carried division with the remainder kept, held so by the three gates of section 8; what changed from the tag v1.1.0 is listed in [CHANGELOG.md](../CHANGELOG.md) and, with the open items by name, in section 10. It stands beside [ALGEBRA.md](ALGEBRA.md) (the law) and [HIGHLIGHTS.md](HIGHLIGHTS.md) (the decisions in force). The paper's engine is the tag v1.1.0 (the freeze at 441b2399), run from the tag and not updated: the lines of the law entered after the freeze, the clock family on the Ports the first, are not implemented in it, and its known issues stand in section 10. The law, docs/ALGEBRA.md, and the paper are the living documents. The engine is the law's implementation and nothing else (by decision): a lattice of Nodes, every family's NodeState at every Node, and one interval of acts in the law's order, each a call of Rule3 (`src/event_universe/core/rule3.py`; Rule3, named for the three directions of the vector it works with, the Link's paces: one read coefficient per Port, R_ij = 2 num p_i^2 Q_ij on the Link to that neighbour, the product of the Node's pace squared, p_i = p_0^2 / Gamma from the Node's own content, the composed clock p_0 = Gamma (1 - 1 / Gamma)^c twice (`src/event_universe/core/paces.py`, the law's two functions of the content), and the Link's factor Q_ij, the Link's tension booked squared as one integer per Link in the unit G^2 of the run's Link unit G, read the same from both ends (ALGEBRA.md #the-paces, The clock is the Node's, the tension is the Link's); the six Ports the three directions twice; three levels in time) on whole-board arrays, every neighbour read through a Port (`src/event_universe/core/ports.py`). The engine holds no number, no formula, no family's name and no flag: every physical value comes from the run's files. No act of the interval takes a square root and no act reads a Node to decide anything: the guard reads the whole initial state once at load, as squares. Beside Rule3's acts the loop takes the NodeDetectors', from outside the Node as the lay and the receding face are: where the world declares the `draw` the NodeDetectors draw inside the run, window after window, and write their click on the lattice at one Node (`src/event_universe/credit.py`, `features/click`; HIGHLIGHTS.md, the engine, A click writes on the lattice: after a click the paths are cancelled on the lattice, keeping Rule3); the Node knows nothing of it, and a world without the key runs as before bit for bit. The repository holds the engine, the tools that lay, run, gate and read a world, and the worlds that gate the engine, the two slits and Bell, with the GHZ gate beside Bell's, the same reader over three sides (HIGHLIGHTS.md, the runs and the worlds, **One test world and one gate**); sections 4 to 6 are the input and the output explained with the two slits and Bell as the examples, and section 9 is the recipe for a new world as the shipped folders have it.

## 1. The words

- **Node**: one place of the lattice. Its whole local information is its **NodeState**, the law's own numbers and nothing else (section 2).
- **Port** and **Link**: a Node has six Ports, +x, -x, +y, -y, +z, -z; a Link is the pair of Ports between two neighbours. A level reaches a neighbour only through a Port (`arrival`). An axis is periodic (it wraps), closed (a wall, read as 0) or open (a wall read as 0 whose outer layer of `face_depth` Nodes is told to report); an axis of one layer folds, its two Ports returning the Node itself. An inner face is the same rule declared inside the board (`faces` in the world file, section 4): a plane of Nodes across an axis, beyond the board but for its gaps; a Node beyond the board is read as 0 through every Port and reads 0 through its own (`core/ports.py`, `Wrap.beyond`), so no level, current or source crosses its Links, nothing is laid or sourced there, and no body, packet or NodeDetector may stand there (refused by name). A **receding face** is a face of an open or closed axis the world declares receding (`receding`, section 4) with the axis's largest size and the layers per growth, both the file's numbers: beyond it the lattice grows by that many layers of zeros whenever a level other than 0 of any family stands on the layer before it, so no wave meets it (`src/event_universe/growth.py` (struck 2026-10-24, the sweep's second commit, the torus the board; kept by name); ALGEBRA.md, **The unbounded board**); with the axis at its largest size the run ends there, named, rather than reflecting; a face before the origin (the low side) grows by prepending, and every declared coordinate (a body's Nodes, a packet's envelope, a NodeDetector's positions, an inner face) stays the file's by the offset of the layers grown before the origin, held once per axis (`Lattice.offset`, read by `mask`; every declared Node is read on the board through `front.on_board` and returned to the file's coordinates by `front.declared`, the one offset in both directions).
- **Lattice**: the torus of Nodes, `shape` [X, Y, Z] as declared, every axis wrapping (the open and closed faces, the inner faces and the receding faces of the record worlds left 2026-10-24, the owner's word: the torus is the board); in code `Lattice` (`src/event_universe/lattice.py`).
- **Family**: one kind of physical value, a row of the universe file: its name, its pair [num, den], its reads derived (2026-10-24, the owner's order, no declarations: every family of quanta and every holder of the volume reads every holder of the volume at 1, every plane every holder of the sign at 1 too, the holder of the sign none, `loader/derived.with_reads`; to that day its `reads`, the holders it read by name each at the integer weight it read with and, by the held write's reciprocity, sourced it with, an empty object where it read none, required, kept by name) and either its dimension (`dimension`, a family of quanta: any integer from 1, the lines of its record, each stepped by Rule3 as a line of dimension one, the form and the share the sum over them; 1 one real line, 2 a plane of two lines, re and im, charged matter, the one dimension with a Wronskian and a turn, 3 three real lines, neutral, which read none of the sign (`loader.universe.shape_of`); or the shape by name, a list of its lines' kinds, `real` or `plane` (1 and 2 kept as spellings; ["plane", "plane", "plane"] a record of three planes, one Wronskian per plane summed into its row of the sign, the proton's row, laid by the generator's one-Node declaration every plane alike; a record of real lines and planes together refused by name, not built); or its shape [parts, dimension], that many records of that dimension laid as one event and never summed at a Node: the pair family [2, 1], two real lines of light's pair, ALGEBRA.md #the-click-is-the-meeting) or, where it is held, no `dimension`: a row without one is a holder, derived from its pair (2026-10-24, `loader/universe.sign_holder` and `volume_holder`, the two holders by their roles since the family-less file of 2026-10-24, both at the massless point (den, den), the gap gone for good on the owner's closure; the former `holder_of`, which read the role from the pair, kept by name; with a gap the holder of the volume, sourced by its readers' form and tensions, 1 + 3 lines; massless the holder of the sign, sourced by their Wronskian and acting by the rotation, 1 + 3 lines; the level weight 1 and the write weight 1, the sign holder's alpha inside its write, no rest; to that day the key `held`, kept by name: `held` {`sources`: what sources it, `["form"]`, `["form", "tensions"]`, `["wronskian"]`, `["form", "tensions", "flux"]`, `["form", "tensions", "shear"]` or `["form", "tensions", "flux", "shear"]`, one real line per source (the flux and the shear three lines each); `level_weight`: the row's level weight E_s, the number of quanta of form that write one level of the row (gravity's the level weight LW of the law, ALGEBRA.md **The two rows**); `write_weight`: the row's write weight k_w, the signed integer multiplier of its one write per line (the sign holder's declared coefficient sits in the write, by decision; a nuclear holder's W_1 or -W_2), 1 in every shipped universe file but the charge's 4 in `examples/events/charged.json` and 400 in `examples/events/turning.json`, the energy line's k_w at T 36,000, every `reads` weight 1; on the massless row holding the content optionally `rest`, the vacuum content c_vac, the level at which it rests everywhere, up to every face and beyond it, 0 by decision, ALGEBRA.md #what-is-open, item 22; `act`, how the row acts on its readers, required on every held row (the file states it, no act by omission): `pace`, the plain read of its level into their paces (every holder of the content's), or `rotation`, the phase of every two-part reader's record on its Links by its level's difference across each Link, the phase line at the fixed angle 1 / Gamma per act (features/phase, section 3; the first build's turn of the record at the Node by the angle with tan(theta / 2) = its level over 2 Gamma scaled by the reader's clock, gone with V2), under which the row carries three odd axis lines beside its time line (section 3; ALGEBRA.md **The sign holder rotates the two-part record**, the engine's declared act)}.). The rule derives everything else from the pair and the shape with no name ([ALGEBRA.md, every family has a dimension](ALGEBRA.md#a-familys-declaration), `src/event_universe/loader/derived.py`): a held row with a gap holds the volume and steps at the paces of its read, every holder of the volume among its reads and its own level among them (to 2026-10-24 the holders its declaration named) (ALGEBRA.md #the-paces, Every row reads the content; its lines the time line alone or with the three axis lines, the parts Rule3 reads and no other); a holder of the content carries no count, no current and no click (a declaration: the engine reads currents, shares and click lines for the families of quanta and the holder of the sign alone, `Lattice.order`, a holder's one region reading the squared deviation of its time line, section 5; the holders' travelling events as records with a count would be a design round of its own, not a fix); a held row sourced by the Wronskian holds the sign and carries quanta, its time line its record, light, alone under the plain read and with the three odd axis lines x, y, z under the rotation; every other family carries quanta (its count is its record's share and its currents the NodeDetectors' reading, readings of the record); every family reads the holders the rule derives for it at the weight 1 (ALGEBRA.md, The reads are derived, 2026-10-24; `derived.with_reads`; to that day the holders its declaration named at the weights it named, `derived.read_of`), a holder of the content plainly into its pace, a holder of the sign under the act `pace` as the size of its level, |L|, a hollow whatever the reader's sense (ALGEBRA.md row (f); `features.read.plain`) or, where the holder declares the rotation, as the turn of its two lines by the holder's signed time level at the Node and by the odd line of each Link it reads through, a plane's read alone (a family of real lines naming a holder under the rotation is refused by name), the holder of the sign never its own level (light reads none of the sign; a holder of the sign naming itself is refused by name), a family naming no holder reading none (the plain rule at Gamma), and each family of quanta sources what it reads at the weight it reads with, by the held write's reciprocity (the form and the tensions for a holder of the content, the Wronskian and, under the rotation, the sign's current, the mean of the Node's two a-Links' Wronskian currents, for the holder of the sign); a second level pair laid on a family whose lines are no plane is refused by name; a packet or a body is laid on every part of its family alike (the symmetric lay, `Lattice.lay`) and on every real line of a part at the line's declared weight (`weights`, the world file's key on the body or the packet, one integer per line, 1 on every line without it, `loader.keys.weights_of`: a record of three real lines laid at (a, b, c) over its lines, the pair times each weight; a weight 0 lays nothing on that line and the record stays whole for every read and every click (2026-10-21)); between clicks no family gives another anything; at a click the conversion lays a credited quantum of one family as whole quanta of other families (`conversion.converted`, `meeting.alone`; ALGEBRA.md, The conversion).
- **Exchange** (the key `exchange`): the family's declared sign under the exchange of two of its parts, `antisymmetric` or `symmetric` in the universe file, undeclared the lists and the credit as they stand; read by the lists' factor beside `full` and by the joint credit as the determinant or the permanent of the sides' parts, the antisymmetric sign read at other than two sides refused by name at load (the determinant is two sides'), nothing at the Node (act 6; ALGEBRA.md, Spin, closed for the paper as a route, The family's sign under exchange).
- **Record**: a line of the books, never a level of its own: a family's level at a Node is one number. The holder of the sign is one record of dimension 1: its time part is its real level pair, the light's record on its free row 0, the row no body owns (light its own family, the architecture of 2026-10-19 at this line's end); its odd axis lines under the rotation are the phase on the Links and no record. **The sign's rows** (ALGEBRA.md, No record reads its own write of the sign, the first of the atom's two lines): a family that reads a holder of the sign, a plane, is a charged family, and every body of it in the world file is its own record, with its own lines (`loader.derived.with_records`, the key `records` of the derived family; a packet of such a family, a packet and no standing record, adds into its first record); every holder of the sign carries one row per charged record of the world beside the free row 0, which no record owns (the laid light's row, the one row where the world has no charged body), each row the holder's lines, its time line and under the rotation its three odd lines (`records.record_slice`), the row of a record the same number in every holder of the sign (`derived.row_of`: 1 + the charged records before it in the file's order); the rows per record are the sign holder's by the law's line and no other holder's (the `wronskian` branch of `with_records`; a holder of the content, the gapped nuclear holder among them, keeps one row, which every record of its readers reads with its own write in it, the Hartree pair with the self-term; the rows per record of a content holder, designed, read cold and given up by the owner's word of 2026-10-19, ALGEBRA.md, The nuclear holder). A record's Wronskian and its sign currents are written into its own row only (`derived.row_sources`: the one record that owns the row; none for the row 0), and a record's read and its turn take the sum of every sign row but its own (`records.row_levels`), so no record reads its own write of the sign and the Hartree self-term is gone, exactly; the holder's level as the board reads it whole is the sum of the rows (`records.rows_total`), the light's record the free row 0 among them (light its own family, 2026-10-19): a NodeDetector, a body of one line and the books read that sum, and the holders of the content are sourced by the form of that sum (the cost, so the Wronskian write's balance stays exact). The second cost, stated: the rows at a Node number the world's charged records, so the bound of local information is the world's declaration (the NodeState of the holder grows by one row per charged body) and not the law's. The law's count 1 per record is the loader's gate (`loader.world.bodies_of`): a body of a charged family is one quantum of its family, one Node with the count 1, and a count above it is refused by name, the refusal naming the way to declare many quanta, one body of count 1 per quantum, each laid by the generator as the one-Node record of its quantum (the generator's one-Node declaration, the design file's `pixels`) and its own record with its own row; the four charge worlds of 10,000 quanta per record left the repository with the count-1 gate by decision (section 6), and the tests' chain body of the charged family is one quantum of count 1, laid as the one-Node record (`tests/laws.py`). Light its own family on every world, the atom world's architecture (the owner's word of 2026-10-19, the branch claude/atom-events; ALGEBRA.md, The emission of a bound record is the click's act of the taker): the family a click names, a transition's `drive` or a rate's `gives_to` (and a conversion's record out, to 2026-10-20), is a family of quanta or a holder of the sign that no family reads, whose one row is the free row 0, the light's record (the two slits' `charge` is such a holder; the Zeno worlds' `pulse` and the shelved ion's drives were, their worlds out of the tree 2026-10-20), and never the holder a charged family reads, whose rows beyond the free row are its records' own writes, refused by name (`loader/node_detector_declaration.lit_families`, the dict a body's declaration names its families from); this line re-read so: the sign holder's time part is the light's record on the free row 0, the row no body owns, and the light a charged body gives is laid as light's own record by the emission act, as the engine lays it (`emission.emitted_quantum` on the `gives_to` family, the source in time inside a guide and the packet on the open board), so no clicked record's family carries a body's static part, and the three findings of the atom's rounds are closed by it (ALGEBRA.md, the same paragraph).
- **Body**: the initial state the world file declares and no ledger: its family, its Nodes with their counts, the counts checked at the start against its record's share in quanta at those Nodes (the gate, section 3). Its Nodes at a later interval are where its family's share stands in quanta about its declared Nodes, derived when a report needs them (`reports.standing_nodes`) and never kept; its quanta that leave it stay its family.
- **NodeDetector**: the record worlds' declaration kind (ALGEBRA.md, The NodeDetector is one declaration kind for every world), in two forms to 2026-10-24: the declared region (`node_detectors`, a connected region of Nodes reading the net current through its boundary Ports, its sides' settings and the world's `draw`) left the tree that day with the sides' meeting that runs back (`cone.py` (struck 2026-10-24, the sweep's third commit, the regions and the run back; kept by name), `meeting_back`'s sides, `lists.sides`, `credit`'s regions and windows; the owner's word: the detectors go, every Node reads itself); the record declared a NodeDetector at a Node (`bodies`, section 4) left the tree the same day with the sweep's fourth commit, with its declaration, its draw, its window, its lists, its guide clause and its resonance (`node_detector.py` (struck 2026-10-24, the sweep's fourth commit, the declared detectors; kept by name), `lists.py` (struck 2026-10-24, the sweep's fourth commit, the declared detectors; kept by name), `guide.py` (struck 2026-10-24, the sweep's fourth commit, the declared detectors; kept by name), `resonance.py` (struck 2026-10-24, the sweep's fourth commit, the declared detectors; kept by name), `loader/draw.py` (struck 2026-10-24, the sweep's fourth commit, the declared detectors; kept by name), `loader/window.py` (struck 2026-10-24, the sweep's fourth commit, the declared detectors; kept by name), `loader/node_detector_declaration.py` (struck 2026-10-24, the sweep's fourth commit, the declared detectors; kept by name), `loader/node_detector_rows.py` (struck 2026-10-24, the sweep's fourth commit, the declared detectors; kept by name); the owner's word: the detectors and the emitters go). No declaration kind stands since 2026-10-24: every world is the universe, the board and the run's length, and no key of the world names a reader or an emitter. The board reads itself (`meeting_board.py`): every Node clicks reading the light at itself.
- **Packet**: a record the world file laid and no body (`packets`), left with the generator on 2026-10-24 (the owner's word, the generator goes; the key is refused as unknown and the record folders' files keep it as frozen text). To that day: a packet of a family of quanta along an axis, its wave number a fraction of pi per Link, its amplitude and its envelope declared, its levels the generator's (`tools/pixel_mode.py`, the packet lay; for a massless family the uniform mode's content taken out of each level by the one lay act's division act, `lay.corrected` through the generator's `packet_levels`, so that now and before each sum to 0 over the board and the massless row's double root at wave number 0 carries neither level nor velocity, ALGEBRA.md, The packet lay; the exact before level of a one-way packet is not compactly supported (a real packet moving one way is an analytic signal, Paley-Wiener; the laid pair's backward root stands at the analytic packet's own leakage into the mirror bins, a few 10^-4 of the form for the two slits, 3.6 x 10^-4 at the weight sin omega and 2.3 x 10^-4 at sin^2 omega), sqrt(1 - **L**^2) having the kernel of |q| / sqrt 3 near q = 0, so a packet whose quadrature sums to S level units carries a before level of order S / (2 pi sqrt 3 r^3) at r Links, of one unit at twenty Links for the two slits; the generator lays it on the board at its largest declared extent along a receding axis and crops to the board; the lattice refuses by name at its construction, through the one lay act's guard, a massless packet whose level sums are not 0; a tool that loads the world without building its board refuses nothing of this); or the same lay on a holder of the content, a kick: the row's own travelling events added to its rest after the start, no count and no source (`tests/test_the_node.py`, the vacuum content's test).
- **Interval**: one step of the whole lattice; `intervals` intervals make a run.

| `phases` (each line `now`, `before`, `remainder`) | the holder of the sign under the rotation | per row of the sign beyond the free row, six lines of the kind "phase": the cosine and the sine line of each axis's Link at the Node owning it (the +a Link of i), Rule3's rotation act at the fixed angle theta_0 = 1 / Gamma (D = 2 Gamma^2, K_0 = D - 1), seeded at the amplitude X the width derives ((X, X cos theta_0, D div 2) and (0, -(X div Gamma), D div 2), `features/phase.seed`, `phase.amplitude`), iterated |n_ij| acts per interval in the sense of n_ij = L(i) - L(j), the row's reader's potential difference across the Link (`node.phased`), and read by the Port as the hop's pair (X cos theta, X sin theta) inside the sign's fold (`plane.phase_links`, `plane.link_pairs`); 18 integers per Node per record; empty for the free row and for every other family (the temporal gauge: the time Link carries no phase, the time turn's shears gone) |

The engine's step receives lines with their coefficients, their sources and their readers and knows no family, no dimension and no name; the lattice groups the lines into families as the loader derived them, for the readings and the sources alone. No count, no sense and no remainder of either stands at a Node: a family of quanta's count is its record's share, e_i = [w (now^2 + before^2) - S now before - now SUM over the six Ports of R_ij before_j] div (2 p_i^2 G^2) at the paces of the read, the conserved form's term at the Node with the weight 1 / p_i^2 of the Node's pace over the vacuum's 2 G^2 (the plain share 3 den (now^2 + before^2) - num now S_6(before) in the vacuum, ALGEBRA.md #the-conserved-form), in quanta (e_i + W_c div 2) div W_c over the wall W_c = 3 den T (`share.share`, `share.family_share`, `share.quanta_of`, `Lattice.share_of`, `Lattice.quanta`); its currents are F_ij = num (now_i before_j - before_i now_j) through each Port, every line's added (`readings.currents_of`, `features/currents`); its form D_i = now^2 - next x before summed over its lines and its Wronskian W_i, the bilinear of a plane's two lines (the integer 0 for real lines, the pair family's among them), are read about the step (`records.form`, `records.wronskian`), the writes' sources, its sign current, the mean of its two a-Links' Wronskian currents, J_a / 2 with J_a = Im(conj(z_i) (z_(+a) - z_(-a))) = re_i (im_(+a) - im_(-a)) - im_i (re_(+a) - re_(-a)) = (G_(i, i-a) - G_(i, i+a)) / num, the net of the conserved current through the Node's two a-Ports from the levels now, the mean (J_a + 1) div 2 rounded as the read rounds the Link's tension, odd under the sense within that unit (`readings.sense_current_of`), the odd lines' source under the rotation, and the well D_i div T is a reading (`records.well`); every one of them read from the record and kept nowhere (ALGEBRA.md #the-count-is-the-records-share; #the-primitives, a family's write is one act). The structural gate `tests/test_rule3.py::test_the_generic_node_is_closed_for_building` holds this table to the code's fields.

## 3. The interval

Before the first interval every held family's time part stands at its sources' rest (the rest solve, `features/start` and `features/start/rests`, runs on no shipped world since 2026-10-24: the massless holder needs no rest solve and `held_rests` returns at once; every row of it rests under the Wronskian of the one record that owns it, the row 0 under nothing, `derived.row_sources`), each at the weight with which the record's family reads the row (the held write's reciprocity), and every held row's record at the start its laid packet plus its sourced rest, the level now and the level before alike (the rest the static solution of the row's line at the paces and the laid packet a travelling one, the line linear at fixed paces, so their sum is the record; the laid levels 0 where nothing is laid, the record then the rest, and a holder of the sign nothing sources stands as laid), over the write's wall E_s T, scaled per proper volume and per proper interval at the paces of the rest as the write scales it; the share in quanta of the laid record is the gate's reading and no source: a body laid by the generator as the standing record of a well sourced by its share stands in a shallower well here, the binding holder's level at the pixel's centre 725 against 870, and its declared count, the generator's reading at the deeper paces, is admitted only within the gate's rounding); within each booking every holder of the content at the rest of its own line at the paces its reads give it, the holders its declaration names among the content at their weights, its own level among them where it names itself (`settled_rows`, `read_content`: the holders' rests taken in turn until every row's levels repeat, or the rows together repeat an earlier state one unit off, a rounding tie, under the same rule; each row's rest the division act iterated from nothing until the levels repeat, the first state the act returns unchanged, and then refined to the line within one fine unit at every Node by the scaled residual (`features/start`, `settled`, `refined`: the floored iteration stops below the line by up to one fine unit over 1 - rho in the board's lowest mode, rho the row's Jacobi factor, 137 fine units on the open 25-cube at the massless pair, 9 levels at the row's unit 15 there; at the stop the line's residual is read exactly in integers, in [0, W), the same line is solved for the correction with the residual scaled by a power of two F as its source by the same act to its own stop, and the correction is added rounded half up, F the least at or above twice the miss's bound K that the width admits for the round, else the largest it admits with the rounds repeated while F is below 2 K, K the least of two bounds where both hold (`bound_of`, the smaller where both hold): the parabola's (6 div 2) ((n + 2) div 2)^2 + 1 along the longest open or closed axis of n Nodes, the six reads over the axis's two Ports times the half-extent squared, plus one (`PORTS`, `SIDES`; the inverse of 6 b - S_6(b) = 6 along the axis is the parabola 3 x (n + 1 - x), which the massless line at the vacuum's paces returns at or above the unit source), and, where the row has a gap W - 6 R > 0 at every Node, W div (W - 6 R) + 1 at the Node where it is largest (den div (den - num) + 1 at the vacuum's paces); the gap bound alone where every axis of more than one Node wraps, the parabola alone where an inner face is the sink, and a refusal by name where no face bounds it; every number the width's and the board's; re-read levels that return to an earlier state further off, a cycle of two states or more, are refused by name with the pair, the cycle's length and the two levels at the Node, no rest laid), 6 den b = num S_6(b) + 3 den sigma where the content is 0, at a fine unit from the host's width (the file's where the host's leaves none, so a file wider than the host lays the same levels), the sources of either sign or both where the board has a sink or the pair a gap, the source over the row's level weight, refused by name where the content reaches the Link's zero at a Node (`paces.frozen_content`), the row's own pace rounded to 0, a frozen clock, `RestCollapses`, which no body within the width's reach meets under the composed paces, the 50-quanta chain body resting at the content 959, U = 0.16), then the holder of the sign at the rest of its line under the content it reads, none of its own level among it, with or without a gap; the same iteration serves the generator, which calls the engine's own start on each record it lays (`tools/pixel_mode.py`, `start_content`; the booking `booked_sources`, one function for both, taken for every held holder under the rotation after the first interval's phase act with the phase lines returned to the angle 0 exactly, 2026-10-21, so that the Wronskian booked is the first step's and the first write returns the rest; the shipped booking at the seed angle 0 kicked every turned world's row at the first write, 567 levels at the 11-cube atom's centre, 3029 to 3596, closed by name the same day), the count's rest its seed alone, the remainder at the half wall of the rule the row steps by, a row with no source at 0 with the same remainder; every write remainder stands at half its wall from the state's making (`records.empty_state`, `records.write_origins`), the walls derived from the rows (`derived.held_write_of`: E_s T for the time part, E_s x 3 den T for each tension part with the sourcing families' den, their least common multiple where they differ, and E_s T, the time part's own wall, for each odd axis line of a holder under the rotation); **Rule3 in flux form, the law's symbols against the code's identifiers** (ALGEBRA.md #the-line, the flux form; the owner's word of 2026-10-16: the engine matches the law in its implementation): the line w (a_next - 2 a_now + a_before) + r' - r = SUM over the Node's six Links of R_ij (m_ij - a_now) - D_i a_now is written once, in `core/rule3.rule3`, and the step (`flux.py`) hands it the Links' sum gathered Link by Link; one row per symbol. The cost per interval, the median of a run on one host against 2.0.0's step: the time paper's run (400x8x8) 45.8 -> 51.3 ms, +12 percent; pixel (25x25x25) 81.5 -> 69.2 ms, -15 percent; the resident memory unchanged.

| the law's symbol | what it is | the identifier | where |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| a_now | the Node's level the step starts from (the level now forward, the level before backward) | `now` (`level` in the step) | `rule3.rule3`, `flux.step_line` |
| a_before | the Node's other level, left behind (forward) or returned to (backward) | `other` | `rule3.rule3`, `flux.step_line` |
| a_next | the level the step writes | the quotient `rule3` returns, `found` | `rule3.carried_division`, `flux.step_line` |
| r, r' | the remainder before and after, in [0, w) | `remainder`, the second return of `rule3` | `rule3.rule3`, `rule3.carried_division` |
| w | the family's wall, 6 den Gamma^2 G^2 | `wall` | `rule3.wall_of`, `Rule.wall` of `node.rule_of` |
| R_ij | the reach of the Link to the neighbour j, read from the Node's end, 2 num p_i^2 Q_ij G^2 | `reaches` (one per Port in Port order; `reads` where the first form named them) | `rule3.rule_integers`, `Rule.reaches` of `node.rule_of` |
| D_i | the Node's mass, 12 (den - num) p_0^2 G^2, 0 for light | `mass` | `rule3.mass`, `Rule.mass` of `node.rule_of` |
| j | the neighbour at the far end of one of the Node's six Links, the Links in Port order +X, -X, +Y, -Y, +Z, -Z | `port`, the index of the Port into `reaches`, `pairs` and the six arrivals | `core/ports.PORT_SIDES`, `readings.ports`, `flux.link_fluxes` |
| S_i | the own weight of the law's first form, 2 w - D_i - SUM_j R_ij, read by the form's Node term (`rule3.form_term`, `share.py`) and by the loader's rooms; not read by the step | `self_coefficient` | `rule3.rule_integers`, `Rule.self_coefficient` of `node.rule_of` |
| m_ij | the record the Link sends: the neighbour's level, the fill beyond a face or at a Node beyond the board (the rest on a held row's time line, 0 elsewhere), the Node's own level on an axis of one layer, the neighbour's pair turned by the Link's pair on a turned Link, the click's value at a faced Node | `level[far]` / `level[near]`, `fill`, the pair product in `turned_link_fluxes`, `face_differences` | `flux.link_fluxes`, `flux.turned_link_fluxes`, `flux.face_differences` |
| SUM_j R_ij (m_ij - a_now) | the Links' sum, the flux | `flux` | `flux.link_fluxes`, `flux.gathered_line`, `flux.gathered_plane`, the first argument of `rule3.rule3` |
| (R^c_ij, R^s_ij) | a turned Link's pair, p_i^2 (X^c_ij, X^s_ij) | `pairs` (`evens`, `odds` per Port) | `plane.link_pairs`, `flux.pairs_of` |
| F | the digits' lattices at the Nodes of the click's frame, the Node's own gcd(w, D_i, R_ij, R^c_ij, R^s_ij) and each Link's gcd(w, R_ji, R^c_ji, R^s_ji) at the neighbour's Port back across it (one Link and one number, 2026-10-24), read at every Node the board's own read asks (ALGEBRA.md, The click's read of the board; the branch record-speed, 2026-10-24): Rule3's integers of the record's read and its Link pairs made once per record per interval on the very read and given again at every frame of the close, as the records' reads and the share are (features/read `READS`, `read_again`, the architect's economy of 2026-10-24), and the turned plane's room at load once per pair (`functools.cache`) | `found` of `digits.lattice_of`; `digits.ruled`, `flux.pairs_of`; `plane.turned_room` | `digits.ruled`, `flux.pairs_of`, `digits.lattice_of`; `plane.turned_room` under `loader/rooms.width_of` and `loader/derived.amplitude_bound` |
| sigma | the direction, +1 forward, -1 back | `direction` | `rule3.rule3`, `rule3.carried_division` |
| the order of the sum | w (2 a_now - a_before), then - D_i a_now, then + the Links' sum, then + r, the order the amplitude bound is derived for | `wall * (now + now - other) - mass * now + flux` | `rule3.rule3` |
| A | the amplitude bound, the engine's and no line of the law | `World.amplitude_bound`, `loader/derived.amplitude_bound` by `bound_under_rooms` over `rule3.partial_rooms` | `loader/derived.bound_under_rooms`, section 4 |

The law's first form, SUM over the Ports of R_ij m_ij + S_i a_now - w a_before + r, the same integers by the identity S_i = 2 w - D_i - SUM_j R_ij, is written in no file of `src/`; `tools/step_oracle.py` keeps it as `arrivals_rule3`, the step's oracle, and runs the engine against it on a world (`tests/test_step_oracle.py` runs two worlds in the suite); every example world and the time paper's run were run with it, forward and back with the record to the lay.

## 4. The input

A run reads two files, both JSON: the world file and the universe file it names by its repository path (the generator's mode file beside the world, `<world>.mode.json`, was the third input to 2026-10-24: the generator goes, the owner's word of that day, and the loader reads no mode file, lays no body from one and no packet, `loader/mode.py` (struck 2026-10-24, the sweep's first commit, the generator; kept by name), `loader/packets.py` (struck 2026-10-24, the sweep's first commit, the generator; kept by name), `loader/lay.py` (struck 2026-10-24, the sweep's first commit, the generator; kept by name), `loader/instrument.py` (struck 2026-10-24, the sweep's first commit, the generator; kept by name) and `frame_run.py` (struck 2026-10-24, the sweep's first commit, the generator; kept by name) gone with it). `src/event_universe/world_files.py` (`load_world`) reads them and `src/event_universe/loader/world.py` (`parse_world`, with `loader/universe.py` for the universe file, `loader/keys.py`, `loader/faces.py` (struck 2026-10-24, the sweep's second commit, the torus the board; kept by name), `loader/draw.py` (struck 2026-10-24, the sweep's fourth commit, the declared detectors; kept by name) and `loader/node_detector_declaration.py` (struck 2026-10-24, the sweep's fourth commit, the declared detectors; kept by name)) checks every key and refuses every other key as unknown by name; no default is written, and a refusal names the key, the value and the reason (`REFUSED` in the output, section 5). A file's integer is bounded by 2^512 (`loader/keys.FILE_INTEGER`, 2026-10-24: the den enlarged as content, 70 bits at the top tier; the host's 63 bits to that day, refusing it), the work path's own choice of the hardware's integers standing where it is (`loader/world.kind_of`).

its folder `nuclide/` (the design, the builder, the world `free_nuclide.json` with its mode file, three beams at k = pi / 4 toward three counters on a square board of 33 x 33 Nodes, and the blind, the two parts bit-identical and every inflow even) left the repository by decision, not in the paper, its reading standing in the repository's history by name. The frozen proton's folder, `frozen_proton/` (a design, a blind and a reading, no world shipped), removed by decision, what does not load is deleted. `neutron_conversion/` (`nucleons.json`, the rule's rows with the rows `neutron` [-4800, 6000] of three real lines, `proton` [0, 6000] of three planes reading the sign holder, `electron` [4000, 6000], matter's plane reading the sign holder, and `antineutrino` [6000, 6000], one real massless line; two findings by name standing: the records out laid by the count stand at one Node whatever their family, so the rotations' sum is not readable there against the neutron's 2.498 and the energies out read the levels' quarter turn and no frequency (the rotations' sum a packet's reading, the packet round's), and the credit's count of the cut body laid by the invariant reads 0 against the reader's 1; the third, no sense laid by the count, closed by round F, the given planes laid on their two lines with the sense the table declares per record out (the world's `to` entries, the proton -1 and the electron +1), every plane alike, the Wronskians -15,987 and 16,384 at the Node at the conversion and the sign rows written from it on, the blind and the reading in the section of that name of `blind_and_reading.md`); the dedicated test runs the committed world at the rate 1 and the window 2 and asserts the lines, the counts, the shares, the lay lines and the crossing, and the sense's test runs it at the rate 1 and the window 1 and asserts the table's refusals, the planes' lines, the Wronskians and the shares at the Node, the twelve lay lines, the sign rows' first write and the crossing. The body round's standing world is not in this tree (its reader `tools/body_standing.py` stays, section 5); the shelved ion's telegraph, `shelved_ion/` (the click round's characterising run: `mercury_ion.json`, the universe of the ion family at the shape [3, 2] and three holders of the sign under the rotation, every family reading nothing on the board; The folders `anticoincidence/`, `zeno/`, `zeno_pulsed/`, `neutron_conversion/` and `shelved_ion/`, the atom folder's `atom_shells.json` and `atom_shells_symmetric.json` and the chain folder's `chain_detuned.json`, `chain_x_plus.json`, `chain_y_plus.json` and `chain_z_plus.json` left the tree 2026-10-20 at the ninth with everything that existed for them alone (the owner's word: what is not needed is deleted; ALGEBRA.md, What a body reads: the toy atom's board carries one bit per line per interval against totals of 54 bits, and their draws before the ninth were the generator's); the paragraph above names them as history of the files at their hash, the universe files a surviving world points at kept in place (`zeno/zeno_atom.json` for the resonance pair and the packet emission).

The complete example, the two slits (ALGEBRA.md row (g)). The world file `examples/events/two_slits/two_slits.json`, its thirteen NodeDetectors abridged to the first screen region and the bare region behind one gap:

```json
{"shape": [45, 48, 1], "boundary": {"x": "open", "y": "open", "z": "periodic"}, "face_depth": 1,
 "faces": [{"axis": "x", "at": 20, "gaps": [{"y": [17, 19], "z": [0, 0]}, {"y": [29, 31], "z": [0, 0]}]}],

               "top": {"x": [10, 10], "y": [4, 44], "z": [0, 0]}, "edge": {"x": 6, "y": 2, "z": 0}}],
 "node_detectors": [{"name": "screen_0", "positions": [[44, 0, 0], [44, 1, 0], [44, 2, 0], [44, 3, 0]]},
                  {"name": "screen_1", "positions": [[44, 4, 0], [44, 5, 0], [44, 6, 0], [44, 7, 0]]},

 "receding": {"x": {"sides": ["low", "high"], "largest": 400, "layers": 16}},

```

A flat board of 45 x 48 Nodes (z of one Node folds), open on x and y with the faces' layer one Node deep; the wall an inner face at x = 20 with two gaps of three rows, y = 17 to 19 and 29 to 31; one packet, a packet of light (`charge`, the holder of the sign, whose record is light) along +x at the wave number pi / 4 per Link (lambda = 8), the level 1,241 at its top (1,328 before commit B, main 7756546d), the top the column x = 10 over the rows 4 to 44 with a raised cosine of six Nodes along x and two across; no body; the screen the column x = 44 in twelve regions of four rows, `screen_0` to `screen_11`, backed by the receding face of the x high side, which grows the board by sixteen layers of zeros whenever a level stands before it, up to 400 columns, so that what passes the screen leaves and never returns, and the source's face, the x low side, receding the same way, so that the wall's return leaves the declared board and nothing passes the gaps a second time (the meeting round's fix, ALGEBRA.md row (g)); the bare region `gap` of sixteen Nodes behind the lower gap, read beside the screen; 130 intervals. The universe file `examples/events/light.json`:

```json
{"integers": {"node_clock": 6000, "quantum_action": 32768, "width": 63, "link_unit": 16},
 "families": [
  {"name": "gravity", "pair": [6000, 6000], "reads": {"gravity": 1, "binding": 1},
   "held": {"sources": ["form", "tensions"], "level_weight": 1000000, "write_weight": 1, "rest": 60}},
  {"name": "charge", "pair": [6000, 6000], "reads": {"gravity": 1, "binding": 1},

  {"name": "binding", "pair": [2400, 2401], "reads": {"gravity": 1, "binding": 1},
```
