
Gamma = 6,000, T = 32,768 and the Link unit G = 16 at the width 63 (the hardware's integers; the amplitude bound 6,949, 9,266 under the first form's total until 2.1.0); gravity, the massless row holding the content with its three tensions at the level weight 10^6 and the vacuum content 60; the charge, the holder of the sign at the level weight 1, its quanta light; the binding holder, a gapped pair holding the content at 10^6 (the two level weights of 10^6 the LW for a light-only world, so that light's own well stays under one level); matter, one real line, laid by no body here; every row reading the two holders of the content at 1 (the charge, light's own row, none of the sign) and every write weight 1; the file as written to 2026-10-24: since, a row carries its name, its pair and its dimension alone and the loader refuses `reads` and `held` by name (section 4), so this universe, its gravity massless, is the record of the law to that day. The engine start file `examples/events/engine_start.json` is `{}`, an empty object (`{"mode": "check"}` until 2026-10-20, the cleanliness audit). The mode file `two_slits.mode.json`, written by the generator (`PYTHONPATH=src python tools/pixel_mode.py --input examples/events/two_slits/two_slits.json`), holds `world_digest` (the world's SHA-256, `7cc634ab...`), `bodies` [] and one entry under `packets`: `family` "charge", `pair` [6000, 6000], `moving` {`now`: {`at`: [344 flat indexes], `values`: [344 levels]}, `before`: the record one interval earlier, the exact lay at the paces of the declared rest, 1256 nonzero Nodes against `now`'s 344}, `amplitude` (the largest level laid under the envelope at the world file's `amplitude`, 1241 since commit B, main 7756546d and 1328 before, the design's number; the lay is the wave under its envelope at the paces of the vacuum, ALGEBRA.md #the-generator (h)) and `count` 1809 (the share in quanta the laid record reads over the board at the paces of the declared vacuum content 60, the generator's lay at those paces; 1804 at main 7756546d, the exact before level at the amplitude 1241 at the content 0; 1805 at main d00aa9e2, 1998 before the lay's fix; the books' count read once over the board). The blind expectation `expectation.json`, written by the builder before the run from the design's numbers alone and read by `tools/click_counts.py` (section 5): `verdict` "NODEDETECTOR", `node_detector` the twelve regions' names, `family` "charge", `window` [45, 130] (the intervals the screen is read over, the whole passage: nothing arrives before 45 and the run ends at 130), `across` "y" (the axis the regions are ordered along), `pattern` [1, 10] (the regions whose extrema are read), `spacing` 16, `seed` 24 (the draw's seed), `counts` [21.0, 20.0, 26.0, 15.0, 9.0, 40.0, 45.0, 16.0, 11.0, 25.0, 22.0, 25.0] (the blind row per region, the Huygens sum summed by four), `through` and `quanta` 273.0 (N's blind, the Huygens total; `counts_sum` 275.0, the twelve counts' sum by their rounding), `maxima` [2, 6, 9] (the blind row's own), `central` 6 (the blind central maximum) and `minima` [4, 8] (the blind first minima about it), `visibility` [70.0, 110.0] (the blind [most - least, most + least] at those regions), `laid` 1809 (the books' count of the lay, from the mode file, at the declared vacuum content 60; 1804 at main 7756546d, 1805 at main d00aa9e2 and 1998 before the lay's fix), `arrival` (the arrival wager in the engine's interval labels: `peak` 69, `centroid` 74 and `span` [63, 77], against the continuum's 63 and 70 and the law's real line's 68 and 73 in the physical labels, with its `status` and its `fence`), `wings` (the regions 3 and 8 at 16.4 and 12.4 against the continuum's 23.7 and 20.4, with its `status` and its `fence`) and `aside` ["gap"] (the bare region read beside the screen). Bell's gate (ALGEBRA.md #the-click-is-the-meeting; HIGHLIGHTS.md, the runs and the worlds, **One test world and one gate**: Bell is the engine's gate and no measurement) is four small worlds of the same kind, `examples/events/bell/bell_a_b.json`, `bell_a_b_prime.json`, `bell_a_prime_b.json` and `bell_a_prime_b_prime.json`, written by `build_world.py` from `design.json` (`--modes` writes the mode files too), one per pair of the CHSH settings (1, 0) and (1, 1) on the left and (12, 5) and (5, 12) on the right: a chain of 61 Nodes (x open, y and z of one Node folded) on the universe file `examples/events/pair.json` (the pair family `light_pair`, light's pair at the dimension [2, 1]); the pair laid as one event at the column 30, two packets of the pair family at k = pi / 4 toward +x and -x with the edge 6 at the amplitude 320 (1,328 until 2026-10-20: the bound gate at load, `loader/draw.compounding_of_the_law`, refuses the a' b and a' b' worlds there, the right's (12, 5) standing on the two-part record, 12 x 1,328 = 15,936 over A = 6,949), the lay the same on both parts; one beam to each side, the regions `left` (the columns 0 to 3) and `right` (57 to 60) backed by the receding x faces (`largest` 400, `layers` 16), each declaring its setting as its `basis`; 100 intervals, the window the whole run. Its blind expectation `expectation.json` is derived from the settings before any run and never touched after: `correlation` E = 119 / 169, -119 / 169, 120 / 169 and 120 / 169 in the CHSH `order`, `S` [478, 169], `marginal` 1 / 2 for each world, the local credits as the fence, `by_the_parts_shares` (S 238 / 169), `by_the_local_sums` (240 / 169) and `by_the_sign` (2), `of_rho` S = (238 + 240 rho) / 169, each with its `status` and its `fence`, the `rule` named (J over the window), `runs` naming the four worlds and `seed` 24; every number an exact fraction.

## 5. The output

The output handed to `Lattice` receives one dictionary per line, every line labelled by its kind (ALGEBRA.md #readings-and-measurements; a Node of the board is read only through a NodeDetector to 2026-10-24, and since that day by the board itself at every Node, `meeting_board`; every number of the output a click or a diagnostic labelled so). The output's words, the lines' keys and labels, the books' keys and the end's, live in `src/event_universe/reports.py` (`OUTPUT`, `BOOKS`, `END`, `reports.click`, `reports.parts`, `reports.density`, `reports.book`, `reports.end`) and nowhere else in the engine: the loader's key table holds the files' words and the reports the output's, and the engine's acts and readings hold no word (the names gate, section 8).

- The `click` line, the measurement (none since 2026-10-24, the declared regions' report left with them; the line as it stood): `event` "click", `label` "NODEDETECTOR", `interval` (the interval), `family` (a family of quanta), `node_detector` (the region's name: a declared NodeDetector's, a body's NodeDetector's, or `face`, the open faces' layer) and `inflow`, the NodeDetector's report: the net current into the region through the NodeDetector's front boundary Ports at its Nodes this interval, inward positive, in the current's units, undivided (N over the family's count wall W_c = 3 den T is the host's reading, `tools/click_counts.py`); the frame's current (ALGEBRA.md, The booking is the frame's, 2026-10-23; the plain current to that day), while the credit's books take the weighted one, each record's frame's current times its Link's factor Q_ij in the unit G^2 (section 3, act 6, `reports.weighted`). One line per interval, family and region where it is not 0; no Node is named.
- The `parts` line (none since 2026-10-24, with the `click` line), the NodeDetector's read of a family of several parts, its content the region's level sums (`reports.level_sums`, a read of the Nodes' levels packaged as the NodeDetector's line, labelled the NodeDetector's since it is read at the NodeDetector's region and nowhere else; the joint shares the credit draws from are accumulated from these sums at every interval and read at the window's close): `event` "parts", `label` "NODEDETECTOR", `interval`, `family` (a family of several parts, the pair family, the GHZ family or the spin branch's two-plane family), `node_detector` (a declared region) and `levels`, per line (one per part of a family of real-line parts, two per part of a family of planes, re then im, 2026-10-19) the signed sums [now, before] of its levels over the region's Nodes at the interval's start; one line per interval, family and declared region where a sum is not 0, no Node; emitted only where the record has several parts or several real lines (`FamilyRule.several`; a record of dimension 3 one line per real line, a plane's two lines, re and im, one line and its sense, not reported), so a world without such a family writes `click` and `density` alone; for a record of several real lines each line's share of the record is read from it as the squares of its sums over the sum of the squares. The reader pairs the parts through the root and squares (`tools/bell_gate.py`). From Bell's run `bell_a_b`: `{"event": "parts", "label": "NODEDETECTOR", "interval": 23, "family": "light_pair", "node_detector": "right", "levels": [[-1, 0], [-1, 0]]}`, the two parts equal, the determinism of equal parts.
- The `credit` line, the click written on the lattice (section 3, act 6), the one click line kind: the board's own click at a Node (`meeting_board.closed`, the Node's index in `node_detector`) alone since 2026-10-24: a body's absorption and emission left that day with the declared bodies (the sweep's fourth commit), the declared region's credit with the regions (the third). To that day a declared region's credit and a record's absorption and emission alike (ALGEBRA.md, The NodeDetector is one declaration kind for every world), the run's reading and nothing else (only the NodeDetector's content is read, the clicks it emits to a file, minding its clock against the board's; the inflow credited is the record's current through the region's front Ports times the Link's factor, `credit.booked` on `node_detector.weighed_currents`, the plain bilinear for a plain record and for a turned record the flux line's own with the Link pairs its step reads, as act 1 reads them, 2026-10-21): `event` "credit", `label` "NODEDETECTOR", `interval` (the board's interval, the board's clock, a diagnostic beside the reader's own), `family` (the record: the region's credited record, or the clicking record's own for a record declared a NodeDetector), `node_detector` (the region credited, or `body n`, the record declared a NodeDetector by its number in the world's order, or, for the board's own report on a world that declares no reader, the Node's x-major index as an integer, 2026-10-24), `window` [first, last] (the board's intervals the draw ran over), `proper` (the reader's proper time at the close in whole intervals, `credit.clocked_regions`, `node_detector.clock_advanced`; the board's interval in the vacuum, less in a well), `windows` (the index of the window closed, the reader's event clock), `before` (the part the record left, by its declared name, for a record's click; None for a region's credit), `realised` (the part entered for a record's click; the port's name, `plus` or `minus`, for a region's credit of a record of several parts; None for one part), `kept` (the parts kept at the written Node for a region's credit of a record of several parts, the others ended; None otherwise), `count` (the quanta moved, 1; 0 at the null window's re-lay), `left` (the count left in the books of the record moved: the credited record's, or the family taken from or given to; None where none), `absorbed` (the family whose arriving quantum was taken, the absorption click; None otherwise) and `emitted` (the family of light a whole quantum was given to, the emission click; None otherwise); and, since the engine V2's Parts F and G and the click's plan, built on the branch claude/click-one-act (2026-10-18), `momentum` (since 2026-10-24 the frame's running cosine along each axis with the Link current's sign, per axis [sign_a, num_a, den_a], p_a = T sin k_a per Link, section 10, row 23; the piece's p_a per axis in T's unit until then), `lost` (the losses booked by name, None where nothing; "no side: booked, not faced" among them where the hole's Node is two-sided on every axis or its Port set's read coefficients sum above the wall, C6), `source` (`body n` or the lay); the keys `fan` (the record's P_a over the board at the close) and `recoil` (the recoil the source owes, -p_a per axis as integers, booked and written nowhere since 2026-10-20, the source's fold per axis until then, Part G; the keys `twist`, n_a per axis, and `shortfall`, s_a = |p_a| - gain_a per axis, of 2026-10-18 gone with the fold) struck from the line on 2026-10-24 (no click reads the board, the click reading the Node and its six neighbours; the recoil -p, redundant with the momentum; section 10, row 23); `bias_bound` (the close's first draw's bound by the read's bases, [the total's gcd with the bases less one, its gcd with the last base, the total], booked by `node_detector.credit_draw` and `node_detector.picked`, `features/click.bias_bound`, 2026-10-20; struck 2026-10-24 with the one-Link read: no base is 0 modulo den and no place value 0 modulo the unit, `features/click.bias_bound` gone, the key kept by name for the logs that carry it) and, on the line of a side of a record of several parts, `order`, `scale` and `board_count` (`reports.ORDER`, `reports.SCALE`, `reports.BOARD_COUNT`); no Node (the reader writes at one Node and gives no result for one Node, the uncertainty principle; the Node written stands in the `lay` lines of the write and the `face` lines of the hole, the host's tool's). One line per click; a world without the `draw` key and without a record declared a NodeDetector writes none. From Bell's run `bell_a_b`: `{"event": "credit", "label": "NODEDETECTOR", "interval": 100, "family": "light_pair", "node_detector": "left", "window": [1, 100], "proper": 100, "windows": 1, "before": null, "realised": "minus", "kept": [1], "count": 1, "left": 197, "absorbed": null, "emitted": null}`, the left side at the setting (1, 0) realising its - port, which reads the part 2 alone (`kept` [1]), the hole at the one Node; a record's absorption carries `before` and `realised` the parts' names, `kept` None, `absorbed` the drive and `left` the drive's count after it. The null window's write, where its re-lay changed a level at a Node, is the same line labelled LATTICE throughout (no quantum passed, `count` 0), the levels in the `lay` lines beside it; a null window that changes nothing writes none.
- The `birth` line, Matter's birth from light at the write step (section 3, act 6; `act.born_pair`, `act.birth_line`; section 10, item 20), written beside the credit line of the emission whose quantum met the standing one and labelled NODEDETECTOR: `event` "birth", `interval`, `family` (the gapless family met), `children` (the plane family's name twice, the conjugate pair), `senses` ([-1, 1] as laid) and `nodes` (the one Node, at the file's coordinates); never a rate, the two `lay` lines beside it the pair's. From tests/test_matter_is_born_from_light.py's small structured board: `{"event": "birth", "label": "NODEDETECTOR", "interval": 45, "family": "light", "children": ["electron", "electron"], "senses": [-1, 1], "nodes": [[2, 2, 2]]}`; on the one world eight such lines at the two absorbers' Nodes.
- The `conversion` line (none since 2026-10-24, with the declared regions), the click of a record converted at a declared region (section 3, act 6; `conversion.converted`, `meeting.alone`, `meeting.met`), written beside the credit line of the quantum it converts and labelled NODEDETECTOR: `event` "conversion", `interval`, `family` (the record in, its count down by one on the credit line; several records met at one Node their families' names joined with +), `node_detector` (the region by name), `window` [first, last], `into` (the families of the records out in the list's order, each up by one whole quantum laid at the credited Node by the count) and `senses` (as laid, the list's turned by the arriving record's own); no Node, the `lay` lines beside it carrying the one Node written. From examples/events/conversion/neutron_chain.json: `{"event": "conversion", "label": "NODEDETECTOR", "interval": 36, "family": "neutron", "node_detector": "target", "window": [25, 36], "into": ["proton", "electron"], "senses": [1, -1]}`.
- The `lay` line, one line of a record laid at one Node by an act from outside the Node (section 3, act 6; `act.written`), a lattice diagnostic for the host's tool and no result: `event` "lay", `label` "LATTICE", `interval`, `family` (the record's), `node_detector` None, `line` (the line's number among the family's lines), `before` and `after` ([now, before, remainder] at the Node before the lay and after it) and `node` {`label` "LATTICE", `at` [x, y, z]}; one line per line a lay changed (the absorption's and the emission's parts, the emitted quantum on light's record at each interval of its span, `emission.sourced`, the null window's re-lay). From the shelved ion's test world: `{"event": "lay", "label": "LATTICE", "interval": 1, "family": "ion", "node_detector": null, "line": 0, "before": [0, 128, 50070528000000], "after": [0, 127, 35914752000000], "node": {"label": "LATTICE", "at": [3, 3, 2]}}`, a null window's re-lay moving a level by the lay's rounding and the remainder to the lay's origin.
- The `face` line (the declared taker's click, struck 2026-10-24 (the owner: no detector); no face line since, the row kept by name), one value presented at the Ports of one Node's set for one interval's step (section 3, act 6; `take.faces_reported`, features/click), a lattice diagnostic and no result: `event` "face", `label` "LATTICE", `interval` (the interval at whose step it is presented), `family`, `node_detector` None, `line`, `ports` (the set in Port order, the hole's the Ports the quantum entered by, from the window's inflow booked per Port, one Port per sided axis and both Ports of a tied axis, kept where the set's read coefficients sum to the wall at most and empty otherwise, the quantum then booked by the deficit path (C6, 2026-10-18 and 2026-10-19, `node_detector.arrival_ports`, `node_detector.face_ports`); a front shell Node's the Ports toward the inner shell, `front.inward`) and `value` (the arrival Rule3 read through each Port of the set in the place of the neighbour's level) and `node`; one line per face, the click's hole over its two intervals and every Node of a front's shell alike. From the same world: `{"event": "face", "label": "LATTICE", "interval": 161, "family": "fluorescence", "node_detector": null, "line": 0, "ports": [4], "value": 34, "node": {"label": "LATTICE", "at": [6, 6, 3]}}`, the counter's hole for one credited quantum at the Node it entered through, at the one Port of its set.
- The `erasure` line (the declared taker's click, struck 2026-10-24 (the owner: no detector); no erasure line since, the row kept by name), the front's shell of one interval (section 3, act 6; `src/event_universe/front.py`), a lattice diagnostic for the host's tool and no result: `event` "erasure", `label` "LATTICE", `interval`, `family` (the clicked record's), `node_detector` None, `origin` [x, y, z] (the click's Node at the file's coordinates), `distance` (the shell's Link-metric distance, the intervals since the click), `nodes` (the shell's Nodes), `take` (the record's share standing on the shell as the front reaches it, in the current's units, what the shell's faces book for removal) and `unit` (the record's quantum W_rec, so that take over unit is the shell's take in quanta). One line per front per interval while its shell holds a Node. With the world's `erasure` L above 1 each shell Node is faced for L + 1 intervals at the fractions (L - 1) / L down to 1 / L of Rule3's own level, then 0 and 0 (`Face.fraction`), the last L shells before the reach falling and every shell behind them at 0; at 1 the two faces to 0 as built.
- The `density` line, a lattice reading and no measurement: `event` "density", `label` "LATTICE", `interval`, `family`, `node_detector` (a declared region; not a body's NodeDetector nor the faces' layer) and `reading`, the family's density over the region: for a family of quanta its share in quanta summed over the region, for a holder of the content the square of its time line's deviation from its rest summed; one line where it differs from the last interval's. Over a region holding a frozen Node (every Link pace 0, where the share is not read and no number is invented, ALGEBRA.md #the-count-is-the-records-share) `reading` is None and the line carries `well`, the frozen Nodes' wells D div T summed, a key of that line alone.
- The books, `Lattice.books()`, a lattice diagnostic: per family of quanta `share` (its share summed over the lattice in the current's units), `quanta` (the same over the family's quantum, `derived.count_wall`: largest_below(9 T^2 (den^2 - num^2)) by `core/paces.sine_den`, the engine's one integer sine, W_c sin omega_0 to the unit, for a family with a gap, W_c = 3 den T for a massless one), `count` (the record's count in the credit's books), `deficit` (the quanta taken from the record and written nowhere less the quanta given into it and laid nowhere, the undepleted beam, `credit.Books.deficits`: the board's share in quanta stands above the books' count by the deficit, the three read side by side at every reading), `drift` (the share against the one the world started with: Rule3's own rounding and the paces' own change over the run), `pace` (the least Node pace p_i = p_0^2 / Gamma of the final state) and `frozen` (the count of its frozen Nodes; `share`, `quanta` and `drift` None while there is one). From the ion-like test world's run of 170 intervals (`tests/test_the_lay.py`, the drive taken three times and written nowhere): `"strong_drive": {"share": 862280640000, "quanta": 1462, "count": 1461, "deficit": 3, "drift": -1306800000, "pace": 6000, "frozen": 0}`, the drift about two quanta of the share, so that `quanta` reads the count plus the deficit within it.
- The end: none since 2026-10-24; to that day `Lattice.ended` named the lawful end at a receding face grown to its largest size (the key `interval`, `axis`, `side` and `largest`), gone with `growth.py` (struck 2026-10-24, the sweep's second commit, the torus the board; kept by name).

`tools/run_inputs.py` (`PYTHONPATH=src python tools/run_inputs.py --out <folder> --jobs 4 <world>.json ...`) runs each world in its own process and writes `<folder>/<world>.output.json`: `input` (the world file's name), `verdict` (`LAWFUL`, or `REFUSED` with `reason`, the loader's, the guard's or the gate's refusal by name at load, or a refusal inside the run with its interval), `intervals` (the intervals run), `ended` (None, or the end's four keys), `books` and `lines` (every `click`, `parts`, `credit`, `density`, `erasure`, `lay` and `face` line in the order they were written); the output carries no time. The two slits' run: `{"input": "two_slits.json", "verdict": "LAWFUL", "intervals": 130, "ended": null, ...}` with 1,209 `click` lines, 134 `density` lines and 278 `credit` lines (the run on main aea3e05) at the interval 130, the window's end.

`tools/pixel_mode.py` (the generator), `tools/standing_lay.py`, `tools/frame_of.py`, `tools/body_rest.py`, `tools/body_standing.py`, `tools/meeting_trials.py` and `tools/click_counts.py` left the tree 2026-10-24 (the owner's word: the generator goes, the detectors and emitters go; the record worlds they built and read stay as frozen files under `examples/`). `tools/bell_gate.py`, `tools/spin_gate.py`, `tools/reading_gate.py` and `tools/look/line_page.py`, the record gates' readers, left the same day (the Bell, GHZ, singlet and slit records stay as files).

The readers, both host tools that compute nothing the files do not declare:

- `tools/eras.py` (`PYTHONPATH=src nice -n 15 python tools/eras.py <world>.json --every 100 --span 50 --out <world>.eras.jsonl`), the eras' log of a world, every board carrying its time line as the volume since the one Node (with `--clicks` the detectors' click, density and detector lines beside it, and with `--nodes` the per-Node log the torus view `tools/look/torus_page.py` draws, 2026-10-23): one JSON line per interval of the board's own readings (`board_readings.eras`: the mean level and velocity with the remainders, the writes per family, the mix's exponent n_eff, the trace's residual R_nat and the volume form's own constraint R_vol = X'^2 / (2 X X'') - 1 from the record's sums over three intervals, the three tension lines' means and velocities, the seed Node against the void; per family of quanta its largest level, its quanta, its share within one Link of the seed, its rulers and its Links' factors at the seed and the far Node), the header naming the held rows' keys, and at the end the estimator of q on the integers' staircase over spans; `tools/look/eras_page.py <world>.eras.jsonl [--blind] [--look] [--notes] [--clicks] [--nodes] [--span] [--out]`, the page that draws the log in page.py's tokens (the eras shaded by the largest write, U and H, q_p by the slope of 1 / v over spans, the writes, the two residuals, the mix's exponent, the seed's share, well, contrast and clock ratio, the rulers, the tension lines and the Links' factors, the quanta per family, the blind's table and the notes; every bang marked and numbered, the seed bang 1 and each later bang the return of the volume's velocity from falling to rising after the fifth act fired since the last, the tops numbered, the horizon's spans shaded with the count of Links held at the wall, and with `--nodes` the least X over the board per interval on a log axis against the floor X_f = volume_of(Gamma, -Gamma) by `swap.floor_of`, the owner's word of 2026-10-23), computing the ratios named and nothing else; `tools/look/torus_page.py` draws every Link held at the wall thick in the hole's colour between its two Nodes, the count in the caption, and its stage strip numbers the bangs and the tops the same way. The shares and quanta these readers take per family after an interval (`Lattice.share_of`, `share.board_share`, `Lattice.quanta`) are read once over the whole board on the records as they stand and given again to every reader of the same records (`features/read.READS` under the key `share`, the architect's second round of 2026-10-24: the eras line, the nodes line and the detector lines read the one share; the same integers); the nodes line's and the detector lines' lists of one number per Node are made by the array and not Node by Node (`eras.whole`, the Link's length once per distinct level), the same numbers).
- `tools/clicks_read.py` (`PYTHONPATH=src python tools/clicks_read.py <world>.clicks.jsonl [--shape X Y Z] [--out <file>.json]`), the reader of the clicks log alone (2026-10-24; ALGEBRA.md, The uncertainty principle is the record's and the click's, The paces compose, The horizon; the builder's rule of method, the clicks log is the record and the eras and nodes lines are looks): from the credit, birth and lay lines and nothing else, no number from a Node's level, the bodies table (the mass from `point`, the reduced pair the click read, T sin omega in T's unit, with `kind` and `sense`, and since 2026-10-24 the rest from the click with no label, 3 cos omega / SUM_a cos k_a = 3 (after + before) / SUM_j now_j, the reduced rational of the temporal pair and the three spatial pairs, 1 for the massless record at every click, None named at a one-Node lay where SUM_a cos k_a = 0, the paces' correction named lacking where `proper` shows the Node's clock off the board's interval; the position from `support` or the board's own click's Node index and the window's close; the velocity from two credits of one `identity` in Links per board interval and per `proper` interval; `momentum`, since 2026-10-24 per axis [sign_a, num_a, den_a], read as p_a = sign_a x T sin k_a per Link in T's unit, the leg of the pair over its den, the word 'no running wave along the axis' where the pair lies outside the circle, the former `fan` and `recoil` struck that day; the energy in whole quanta and in T's unit, the band's form from the pairs directly, no T needed; bound or free with the `beat`), the uncertainty table per family and window (the count's floor, the where's spread per axis on the torus in Links, the momentum's spread in T's unit, their product in Links x T, the violation check against the law's floor T / 2, named in the row) and the GR table (the dilation of `proper` against the board's intervals, light's click spacing per Node, the Nodes silent while others click, a free body's track against the straight line); every cell the log cannot fill names the key it lacks. On the committed record: 125 credits of light at the interval 30, the count's floor 1 over the one-interval window, the where 5 Links on every axis, the momentum unreadable (`momentum` null on every board's-own credit), `proper` the constant 0, the bodies and the GR tables unreadable with their keys named (tests/test_the_clicks_reader.py). Under the click's view (the six readings on the board's own click, 2026-10-24) the reader takes the keys as written: `point` the pair, or the lines disagreeing, or lacking at a level now 0; `beat` the record's own unit beside the mass; the birth line's `records` and `points` as the born bodies, each with its mass and sense and no credit of its own; `proper` the credited Node's clock, so the dilation reads per Node over its successive closes; a record credited at several Nodes in one close named as the credited record and no one body; no T needed, every reading in T's unit from the pairs (`--action` and the reader's `action_of` gone 2026-10-24). The seven tables of the record's checks (2026-10-24, the owner's words of 16:3x UTC: all the epochs since the big bang in the clicks, another bang that erases nothing, the attraction of clicking bodies, relativity and the Links' lengthening in the clicks, one place decoding them by any means; the architect's list; tests/test_the_readers_tables.py): `density_table` (the clicks per interval and the epochs named bang, rise, top, fall and return with their bounds from the eras log's tops and returns, tools/look/captions.py, or the density's own runs where no eras log is given; one cycle with no top and no return named `the first rise alone`), `tracks_table` (per identity the series of its credits, close, where, proper, point and sense, the where the torus coordinates from `support` or the Node's x-major index), `distance_table` (per common close the torus's shortest distance in Links between two tracks' wheres and its slope in Links per interval, the two longest bodies' tracks or two the caller names with `--bodies`; the tracks that stand named, `the one-Node body's group velocity 0`, light's fall from the GR table beside them), `ledger_table` (per cycle the bang, the top, the return, the births per point and sense, the eras log's quanta per family at the top, before the return and at the return, the families erased across it and the tracks whose credits span it, captions.ledger_of's shape), `dilation_vs_clock` (the dilation per where against the pace e^-U from the nodes log's `length` summed over the intervals between the two closes, the difference and whether it lies within one interval, the verdict naming the wheres off), `spacing_vs_length` (light's click spacing per where with e^U at each close, the relation's sign over the board and on both sides of every return) and `births_table` (per cycle per point, named by captions.captions_for, per sense the count, the senses' sum per cycle, the lists equal to the legend's counts); the eras and nodes logs are looks beside the clicks (`--eras`, `--nodes`) and a table that needs one says so where none is given; every table printed beside the three into the `--out` file. On the committed record with its two logs beside it: 26 epochs over 7 cycles, the 125 clicks at 30 in the first rise, the ledger's 7 rows with nothing erased across the 6 returns, 125 proton lists in the births, the tracks and the clock tables unreadable with their keys named.
- `tools/back_in_time.py` (`PYTHONPATH=src python tools/back_in_time.py --intervals 40 <world>.json`), the back-in-time gate of section 3, prints `{"verdict": "MATCH", "intervals": 40, "interval": 1, "ended": null}` on the two slits' world and, with `--intervals 100`, on each of Bell's four worlds and, with `--intervals 60`, on each of the GHZ's four, or MISS with the first interval, array and Node that differ. Since 2026-10-24 the window `--from K` (`PYTHONPATH=src python tools/back_in_time.py --from 380 --intervals 20 <world>.json`): the board runs forward to the interval K keeping no snapshot and no output line, then `--intervals` forward with a snapshot after each and as many back compared down to K, the report as before with K under `from`, so a run of thousands of intervals is checked across a return where every interval's snapshot does not fit (tests/test_the_readers_tables.py).

## 6. How to run a world

1. Write a world file naming its universe file, its shape and its intervals (section 4): the seed is light at one quantum on every Node (`seed.py`); no body and no packet is laid from a file since 2026-10-24 (the builders of the record folders under `examples/` and their mode files stand as frozen records).
2. The generator (`tools/pixel_mode.py`) left the tree 2026-10-24 on the owner's word: no lay from outside the law's one lay act, the seed the engine's own.
3. Run it headless: `PYTHONPATH=src python tools/run_inputs.py --out runs/first --jobs 4 <world>.json`, one output file per world, one summary line printed per world; the two slits' world runs in a few seconds on one core (its 130 intervals, the board grown to 269 columns by the end of the run, the file's largest 400), Bell's four chains in seconds each (100 intervals, the chain grown to 221 Nodes by the end, the file's largest 400), the GHZ's four square boards of 33 x 33 Nodes in a few seconds each (60 intervals, the board grown at its four receding faces).
4. Read the verdict first: `REFUSED` names the key, the value, the guard or the gate that refused; `LAWFUL` carries the lines and the books (section 5).
5. Gate it: `PYTHONPATH=src python tools/back_in_time.py --intervals 40 <world>.json` says MATCH on the two slits' world, printing `{"verdict": "MATCH", "intervals": 40, "interval": 1, "ended": null}` (a run on main 637a062), with `--intervals 100` on each of Bell's four worlds and with `--intervals 60` on each of the GHZ's four, and on the reader worlds across their clicks (`--intervals 60` on `examples/events/bell_singlet/born_a_b.json`, across its clicks; the Zeno and the anticoincidence worlds left the tree 2026-10-20), the faces presented from the `face` lines and the lays undone from the `lay` lines (on the fix round's engine, the nine gate worlds MATCH at 130, 100 and 60 and every number is unmoved, N 278, S 478 / 169, M -4); a reading is read only on MATCH. The click's checks, each one test function: the gate worlds bit for bit through the one act with MATCH on each (`tests/test_the_meeting.py::test_the_meeting_is_the_pairing_through_the_root_and_the_engine_implements_it_exactly`, `::test_the_ghz_gate_pairs_four_parts_through_the_root_on_three_sides_exactly`); the credit at one Node, the inverse across the clicks and the count-0 record credited never with no draw consumed (`::test_the_click_is_written_at_one_node_and_the_board_is_exact_between_clicks`); the absorption, the emission and the null window at one Node, the front's theorem, the back-in-time gate across every act from the lines on the shelved ion's test world and the two shipped reader worlds with the start returned, the booking identity per act at every step (the conversion's list through the one act and the generator's consumption asserted to 2026-10-20, gone with their code), the face lines equal to the books' values and the one-in-flight limit's absence by name (`tests/test_the_node_detector.py::test_a_record_declared_a_reader_over_two_nodes_takes_gives_and_stays`, with `tests/laws.py::booked`); the share identity on the gate worlds and the hand worlds (`tests/test_the_node.py::test_the_interval_on_a_closed_cube_conserves_the_count_keeps_the_48_returns`).
6. The two slits' credit reader `tools/click_counts.py` left the tree 2026-10-24 with the generator (the two slits a record).
7. The shelved ion's telegraph left the tree 2026-10-20 with its folder, and its generator run went with the generator on 2026-10-24.
8. The Zeno world and the anticoincidence world, their builders and their readings, left the tree 2026-10-20 at the ninth with their folders (#2156; section 4, the folders' history); `tools/meeting_trials.py` stands and reads the resonance world below (its coincidence rows derived from the records' count, `coincidence_rows`: the absorptions at every record alone and at every pair and no other, at neither, and alpha per pair; for two records A alone, B alone, both, neither and alpha, a world of three records its three singles and three pairs). Build, lay and run the which-way world: `PYTHONPATH=src python examples/events/which_way/build_world.py --modes` writes the three worlds, their mode files and the blind, `PYTHONPATH=src python tools/run_inputs.py --out runs/way examples/events/which_way/which_way.json examples/events/which_way/one_gap.json examples/events/which_way/two_gaps.json` runs them, and the clicks per region are the `credit` lines per NodeDetector, read beside `expectation.json` as `blind_and_reading.md` reads them. Build, lay and run the resonance world, test (vi): `PYTHONPATH=src python examples/events/resonance/build_world.py --modes` writes `resonant.json` and `detuned.json` with their mode files and the blind, `PYTHONPATH=src python tools/meeting_trials.py --world examples/events/resonance/resonant.json --design examples/events/resonance/design.json --expectation examples/events/resonance/expectation.json` reads the taker's absorptions over the design's seeds, `PYTHONPATH=src python examples/events/resonance/read_world.py` reads both worlds over the seeds under the dark grain (per seed the emission's interval, the lay lines, the far Node's cosine on its plateau and the light's count at the span's end, lattice readings labelled so, with the absorptions), as `tests/test_the_draw.py` reads them over the first seeds and `blind_and_reading.md` records them.
9. The rotation round's look (the like-or-unlike worlds of `examples/events/like_or_unlike/`, two bodies of the charged family on a chain under the holder of the sign's act `rotation`, with their uncharged controls, read by `tools/body_drift.py`) left the repository by decision, the six charged worlds refused at load and the three uncharged ones the control of nothing; its universe file stands at `examples/events/turning.json` for the rotation's tests, and its readings stand in the repository's history by name.
10. See it, a diagnostic: `PYTHONPATH=src python tools/look/record.py <world>.json --intervals N` is the host's reader, which steps the world by the engine's own step and writes every family's arrays per interval (a family of quanta's two levels now, its count as the record's share in quanta, a reading of the record, its share over the lattice in the current's units, the books' per interval, the form the interval booked and the least Node pace of its read; a held family's level and its further parts), each frame over the lattice's shape at that interval with its `shape` and `offset` (the layers grown before the origin, every Node of the frame named at the file's coordinates), the `click` lines, the bodies' Nodes derived (a bound body's light front as `shell` and `radius` on its row, and per frame its own light's book per shell Node with the frame run's F and rho's quotient at the last close as `own_light`, 2026-10-22), the world's declared numbers (the inner faces with their gaps and the receding faces among them), the end where the run ended at a receding face's largest size and the books to `<world>.look.json`, labelled "lattice reading" and holding no number of its own; frame 0 is the world as laid, its counts the share the gate admitted the bodies' declarations within. With `--every K` the look keeps frame 0, the frame of every K-th interval and the last frame stepped (1, the default, every frame), each kept frame the full look's frame at that interval bit for bit with its own `click` and `parts` lines, so that a long run's look fits one page; the intervals between the kept frames stand in no frame, the readings read a full look recorded locally and not shipped, and a page of a strided look takes its measurement from the run's output file (`--output`); the look names its stride in `every` and the summary line its frames. `PYTHONPATH=src python tools/look/page.py <world>.look.json [--blind <expectation>.json]` writes the one page that shows it: the board as cubes in one fixed view, seen along one axis and never turned (a folded axis the one seen along, the board the plane it is; a board of three axes seen along z one layer at a time, the layer picked beside the board; the default camera the board's own fit where it shows a Node at six pixels or more and, since 2026-10-21, the lit box on a long board, the Nodes any family lights over the run with the bodies' and the regions' Nodes (the union of the frames' lit boxes, one scale held frame to frame; the frame's own box until that day's visual pass), whole at three pixels per Node or more, the whole board one zoom step out; a dot's least radius --dot-least pixels at the fit's scale beside --dot-floor in Links (2026-10-21); every bar at the credit's floor), the declared board alone in the frame, the layers a receding face grew on the board's own switch, off by default, the frame's label counting the Nodes with a count out there (2026-10-21), every Node at the file's coordinates, the inner faces the world declares as dark cubes with their gaps open ("the faces (declared)" in the legend), one frame per interval, the counts as dots resting on the cubes, the levels as the cubes' glow, a field as mist, the NodeDetectors' Nodes as rings seen at rest and brighter the interval they report; the NodeDetectors' report as the bar chart, the one measurement: with an expectation in the format `tools/click_counts.py` reads one bar per region ordered along the `across` axis, what it saw within the window summed from its `click` lines as click_counts.py sums them and the rounded shares (N times the shares by the largest remainders, the expectation), the dashed blind curve behind them, the pattern's range marked, the totals line and one watch line per key of `watch`; with an expectation of the NodeDetectors' totals one bar per NodeDetector, and with none the totals as reported; the graphs over the intervals beside it, the first the share per family over the lattice in quanta from the look's books per interval, every family's role from the file's row; every number on the page is the files' or the engine's, the page computes nothing but the sums its bars and graphs draw and, where the blind names a line, the discrete transform of one Node's form over the frames, a reading of the drawn trace (2026-10-20), and only the NodeDetectors' report is drawn as a measurement; the blind's numbers in a table of their own, its words under a fold, and with an expectation across an axis the NodeDetectors' arrival over the intervals as two graphs, the reporters' inflow per interval and the quanta gathered within the window, the blind's peak, centroid, span and total marked where it names them; ## 7. How to add a family

No family is added since 2026-10-24, 09:4x UTC (the owner's word, declaring a family is forbidden; ALGEBRA.md, No family is declared; section 4, the rows derived from den at load): the universe file declares den, the clock and the unit, every row is derived from den at load, and the paragraph below is the law to that hour, kept by name. A family is a row of the universe file, never a line of the engine: its `name`, its `pair`, its `dimension` for a family of quanta, and no `dimension` for a holder (2026-10-24, the owner's order, no declarations; to that day its `reads`, the holders it read by name, each at its weight, `{}` for none, and for a held family `held` {`sources`, `level_weight`, `write_weight`, optionally `rest` on the massless row of the content and `act` on the holder of the sign}, kept by name). The rule derives from the pair and the dimension what it holds, its lines and its reads, each at the weight 1, and the rows it sources follow by the held write's reciprocity; the engine steps it, reads its share and its currents, holds it and reads it with the code it already has; a charged body of a pair is a second row of that pair at the dimension 2; a record of three real lines is a row at the dimension 3, neutral, laid at its declared `weights` over its lines. The relative part or the centre of two bound records is a row declaring its pair as every family does (the loader derived it from theirs, {`relative_of`: [a, b], `den`} and {`centre_of`: [a, b], `den`}, by `composed_pair` until 2026-10-21, deleted 2026-10-21 with no world, its loader test, its no-root assertions and its two acts-table rows gone with it; ALGEBRA.md, The pair of two bound records). A new act of the law starts from its line in ALGEBRA.md, is a pure function of arrays that calls Rule3 alone, and comes with its test; a new reading of the record is a function of the record's arrays and nothing kept beside them.

## 8. The gates

`python tools/check.py` selects the changed files and their consumers and runs `ruff`, `ruff format --check`, `mypy` (strict) and `pytest` on them with the tests of `tools/every_pull_request.txt` (one test file per line, the law's tests, all of them); `--full` runs everything, and CI (`.github/workflows/check.yml`) runs the suite's test functions in five shards balanced on `tools/test_seconds.json`, the seconds per test function re-recorded with `--record-seconds` from a run's junit file, every job under the ceiling of ten minutes (`timeout-minutes`); the suite's bound (a test runs under 30 seconds) is a gate of the plan, `TEST_SECONDS_BOUND`: a test function above it fails `--plan` by name on the recorded table and fails every `check.py` run by name on the run's own junit, so a slow test is shortened to the smallest board and run that hold its assertion and its long reading moves to a tool run by name. With them the ratchets against the merge base (`CHECK_BASE`, else `origin/main`), each one command and no recorded baseline, run by `check.py` on every selection, with `--full` and in CI's last shard, each failing with its own message: `tools/engine_gates.py` (the four gates: no new numeric literal, family name or hand division in the engine beyond the merge base's, and a new module of `core/` only with the `APPROVED-CORE` line in the pull request's body), `tools/record_code_shape.py` (the shape of `src/`: the lines, the docstrings, the sites of Rule3's arithmetic and of the writes, the import contracts) and `tools/tests_shape.py` (the shape of `tests/`: it only shrinks, no copied setup, no history markers beyond the merge base's; a test given room by name stands in the tool's `ROOM` table with its lines and the reason, the room added to the base's size while the test holds it, section 9), all on `tools/merge_base.py`; `tools/documents_gates.py` (the documents' three gates, run by `tests/test_documents.py`: no undated history clause in ALGEBRA.md or this document beyond `tools/history_allowed.json`, a list that only shrinks, each clause dated or struck by a fill; every number of `tools/numbers.json` in one of its canonical forms where the table names it and in none of its forbidden forms, a number that stands in two places being a row of the table; every number the table derives the output of its script under `tools/derivations/` to the digits named, so that a hand's derivation is a script anyone runs; ## 9. How to build a world

The recipe a reader with the repository alone follows to declare a world, write its blind first, run it and read it, derived from the shipped folders of section 4 (the paper's own world, `examples/events/two_slits/`, is the pattern; `examples/events/which_way/`, `examples/events/bell/`, `examples/events/ghz/` and `examples/events/matter_alone/` follow it; `examples/events/chain/` writes its blinds before any run, reads the lay's numbers with no interval stepped and asserts the blinds by a test, the pattern for a blind on the click's conservation), from the loader (`src/event_universe/loader/`, `src/event_universe/world_files.py`), the reports (`src/event_universe/reports.py`) and the tools (`tools/`); every key and command below is the code's on `main`. A world is one folder under `examples/events/` holding its design (`design.json`), its universe file, its world file with the generator's mode file beside it, its builder (`build_world.py`) and its blind (`expectation.json`); the engine holds no number, so every number of the world stands in these files and in nothing else, and the folder is the whole of it.

1. **The universe file**, one JSON object of exactly two keys, `integers` and `families` (`loader/universe.py`, `universe_of`; any other key is refused by name, as everywhere in the files). `integers` holds exactly two since 2026-10-24, each an integer from 1: `node_clock` (Gamma) and `quantum_action` (T); the Link unit G is the engine's, 16 (`core/ports.LINK_UNIT`; the key `link_unit` refused by name since, a power of two declared 16 in every file to that day), and the width (the bits of the run's integers: at or under 63 every array is the hardware's 64-bit integers, above it Python's, exact and about a hundred times slower) is derived from them and the families, the least at which the amplitude bound holds the volume's level at the Link's last pace (`loader/rooms.width_of`; the key `width` refused by name since, 92 in the one universe file and 63 in the rule's to that day); the one universe file declares 1000 and 36000, and a new universe file copies them (`examples/events/rule.json` is the rule's own, at 6000 and 32768 with its width 63 and its Link unit 16 declared, loaded no more). `families` is a list of one row or more (no switch among its keys since 2026-10-23, the law of one Node, section 4), each `name` (its own; a name twice is refused) and `pair` [num, den] with den from 1 and num from -den through den, |num| = den admitted only as num = den, the massless pair (a massive pair has den above |num|: [6000, 6000] is light's, [4000, 6000] matter's, [2400, 2401] the binding holder's, and [-6000, 6000] is refused by name), and then, for a family of quanta, `dimension`, any integer from 1: 1 (one real line), 2 (a plane, re and im, whose body the generator lays with a sense, item 2), 3 (three real lines, each stepped as a line of dimension one, the record laid at declared `weights` over them, naming no holder of the sign), the shape by name as a list of the lines' kinds, ["plane", "plane", "plane"] three planes (the proton's row), or the shape [parts, dimension] (that many records of that dimension laid as one event and never summed at a Node: the pair family [2, 1], the GHZ family [4, 1], the deuteron's `nuclide` family [2, 2]; a shape of three numbers and a dimension below 1 are refused); and no `dimension` for a holder, derived from its pair (2026-10-24, the owner's order, no declarations, `loader/universe.sign_holder` and `volume_holder`, the two holders by their roles since the family-less file of 2026-10-24, both at the massless point (den, den), the gap gone for good on the owner's closure; the former `holder_of`, which read the role from the pair, kept by name: gapped the holder of the volume, sourced by its readers' form and tensions, 1 + 3 lines, carrying its time line as the volume's level; massless the holder of the sign, sourced by their Wronskian and acting by the rotation of their records, 1 + 3 lines, its own record light; the level weight 1 and the write weight 1, the sign holder's alpha carried inside its write, no rest; to that day the key `held`, kept by name and refused since by name with this derivation, `loader/universe.GONE`: `held`, for a held row, which declares no dimension: `sources`, exactly one of ["form"] (a holder of the content), ["form", "tensions"] (the row of the content with its three axis lines, gravity's, massless or gapped, which carries its time line as the volume's level), ["form", "tensions", "flux"] (the same with the flux's three odd lines) or ["wronskian"] (the holder of the sign, whose own record is light and whose act is the rotation of its readers' records), `level_weight` from 1 (the quanta of form that write one level of the row), `write_weight`, a signed integer, on a holder of the content (the write's weight k_w, required there, a holder of the content without it refused naming the family) and refused by name on the holder of the sign (its alpha the ratio of the file's integers carried inside its write, section 4), and optionally `rest` (the vacuum content, an integer from 0 within the width, admitted on the massless row sourced by the form and refused on the holder of the sign and on a gapped row); no `act`: every holder of the content acts by the plain read into its readers' paces and the holder of the sign by the rotation, the law of one Node (2026-10-23; the key of 2026-10-19 to 2026-10-23 refused as unknown)). Who reads whom is derived (2026-10-24, `loader/derived.with_reads`: every family of quanta and every holder of the volume reads every holder of the volume at 1, a holder of the volume its own level among them, every plane every holder of the sign at 1 too, the holder of the sign none; to that day the row's own declaration, the key `reads` required on every row, each holder the family read by name at the integer weight it read with, `{}` for none, kept by name and refused since naming the derivation), and each family sources what it reads at the same weight, the held write's reciprocity. The amplitude bound A is derived from these numbers and never written (section 4), and a universe at which no level fits inside the width is refused by name. A universe file is read only through a world that names it under `universe`, so its first check is the run of item 4; `examples/events/deuteron.json` was the shortest shipped one, two rows and no holder of the content, the loader's tests' universe with no world on it, to 2026-10-24; since, every universe file but the one world's carries the gone keys and is the record of the law to that day, and the tests build their universes in tests/laws.py.

2. **The world file**, since 2026-10-24 one JSON object of three keys, `shape`, `intervals` and `universe`, every one required and every other key refused as unknown (the sweep's second to fourth commits); to that day one JSON object of at most twelve keys, `shape`, `boundary`, `face_depth`, `faces`, `intervals`, `universe`, `bodies`, `packets`, `node_detectors`, `receding`, `draw` and `lay`, of which `shape`, `intervals` and `universe` are required, `bodies` and `node_detectors` optional since 2026-10-24 (a world naming none of the three, `bodies`, `packets` and `node_detectors`, declares nothing, while a world naming them empty declares an empty board as before: its seed the universe's one massless family of quanta at one quantum on every Node and its reader the board itself, ALGEBRA.md, The uniform seed is a lay and The board reads itself; the former requirement of the two keys kept by name; the shipped one world, examples/events/universe24/universe24.json, is such a file), `boundary` optional since 2026-10-23 (the torus without it, the owner's word of that day; the thirteen shipped worlds periodic on every axis declare none) and `face_depth` with an open axis (`loader/world.py`, `parse_world`, `WORLD_KEYS`; the refusal of a thirteenth key lists the twelve; CHANGED 2026-10-23, the old kept by name: fifteen keys with `erasure`, `uniform` and `interval`, the switches of 2026-10-21 and 2026-10-22, gone with the law of one Node; and 2026-10-20: fourteen keys with `engine`, the engine start file's path, required, left with the ninth commit). The board: `shape` [X, Y, Z], each from 1 (an axis of 1 folds, its two Ports returning the Node itself: the shipped flat worlds declare z 1); `boundary` {`x`, `y`, `z`: each `open`, `periodic` or `closed`}, every axis `periodic` where the key is absent; `face_depth` from 1, the depth of the open faces' reporting layer; optionally `faces`, the inner faces, [{`axis`, `at`, `gaps` [{the other two axes: [first, last]}, ...]}, ...] (the two slits' wall; faces that leave no Node are refused); optionally `receding`, {`x`, `y` or `z`: {`sides` [`low` and/or `high`, each once], `largest` (above the shape's extent on that axis), `layers` (from 1)}}, on an open or closed axis of two Nodes or more (a periodic axis has no face), the board nothing returns from (every shipped world recedes to 400 by 16 layers); `intervals` from 0; `universe`, a repository path from the checkout's root (`engine`, the engine start file `examples/events/engine_start.json`, `{}` since the cleanliness audit of 2026-10-20 and `{"mode": "check"}` before it, left the world file with the ninth commit, 2026-10-20). The lays, two kinds: a body under `bodies`, {`family` (a family of quanta), `nodes` [{`node` [x, y, z], `count` from 1; one Node with the count 1 for a family that reads a holder of the sign, one quantum per charged record}, ...]}, the generator's standing lay: the world declares one Node carrying the whole count, and `tools/pixel_mode.py` finds the body's fixed point in the rows its family reads, rewrites the body's Nodes and counts as the record's share in quanta at the paces of the read (the number the gate reads at the start, so the declaration and the reading are one) and writes its levels to the mode file; a body of a plane is laid rotating in the sense the design file's `senses` names for it, +1 or -1, one per body in the world's order; refused by name: a body whose standing rotation is not above its band's top (a cloud, below the window of mass), a body whose iteration drives a pace to 0 (a collapse, above it) and two bodies whose regions share a Node (`examples/events/matter_alone/pixel.json`); and a packet under `packets`, {`family` (a family of quanta, or a holder of the content for a kick), `along` (`x`, `y` or `z`), `wave` [p, q] (k = pi p / q per Link, p from -q through q and not 0, its sign the direction), `amplitude` (from 1 within A), `top` {`x`, `y`, `z`: [first, last]}, the flat top, and `edge` {`x`, `y`, `z`: the half-width of the raised cosine beyond it, 0 none}, `phase` [r, s] (required; [0, 1] for none) and optionally `transverse` {an axis across the beam: [r, s]}}, the generator's packet lay, a real packet with its level one interval earlier, on a family of several parts laid on every part alike, the symmetric lay (the two slits' one packet over the whole cross-section; the GHZ's record of four parts, laid on every part alike); `whole` is a key the parser knows and refuses by name. The NodeDetectors, under `node_detectors`, each {`name` (its own and not `face`, the loader's own row for the open faces' layer), and either `positions` [[x, y, z], ...], one region of the declared NodeDetector, or `block`, a body's number from 0, whose Nodes report; optionally `basis`, a list of integers not all 0, the reader's setting (p, q), and with it `pattern`, one pair [alpha_k, beta_k] per part of the record of several parts the world lays, none [0, 0]} (`loader/draw.py` (struck 2026-10-24, the sweep's fourth commit, the declared detectors; kept by name); a pattern without a basis, of another length or in a world laying no such record, and a basis on a body's NodeDetector, are refused; the two slits' screen regions declare neither, Bell's and the GHZ's regions both). The size rule (`regions_of_the_law`): a region is two Nodes or more, connected through the Links, and at least q / |p| Nodes across the beam of every packet of its family, on every axis of more than one Node other than the packet's axis, the depth along the beam not gated (a counter for a beam of k = pi / 4 is at least 4 Nodes wide across it; a region of 2 is refused by name with the wavelength). A region's whole, the energy the reader divides its inflow by (the screen's W_c = 3 den T, light's quantum), is no key of the world or of the engine: it stands in the design and in the blind (item 3), and the engine credits every region alike in its record's own unit, read once from the lay (`credit.record_unit`): the family's quantum, largest_below(9 T^2 (den^2 - num^2)) = W_c sin omega_0 for a family with a gap, W_c = 3 den T for a massless one; a count is one quantum of the invariant, 2 A^2 sin omega = T per quantum per line (R8). What a world may not contain: a key the table lacks; a Node outside the shape or beyond an inner face, in a body, in a NodeDetector or as a packet's `whole`; two bodies on one Node; a body or a packet without its entry in the mode file beside the world, or a mode file written for another world (the world's SHA-256 digest stands in it, so a world edited after the generator ran is refused until the generator runs again); a second level pair on a family of dimension 1; a level above A. The generator's command: `PYTHONPATH=src python tools/pixel_mode.py --input <world>.json` (the design file beside the world its inputs, nothing on the command line) writes `<world>.mode.json` beside the world and rewrites the world with the bodies' Nodes and counts; a world of packets alone needs it all the same (`examples/events/two_slits/two_slits.json`), and without it the first refusal a new world meets is the mode file's, before any NodeDetector is checked.

3. **The design file and the blind.** `design.json` holds every number of the world and the engine reads none of it: the universe and engine paths, the family, the board's numbers, the lays' numbers, the NodeDetectors with their beams and their wholes, the receding faces, `intervals`, the `window` of intervals the reading runs over, the `seed` of the reader's draw and a `comment` naming the law's lines the blind rests on (the two slits' keys: `universe` and `engine`, `family`, `length` 45 and `height` 48, `wave` [1, 4], `packet` {`column` 10, `edge_along` 6, `across` [4, 44], `edge_across` 2}, `amplitude` 1241 (1328 before commit B, main 7756546d), `wall` 20 with `gaps` [[17, 19], [29, 31]], `screen` 44 and `rows_per_region` 4, `aside` (the bare region `gap`), `receding`, `intervals` 130, `window` [45, 130], `pattern` [1, 10], `spacing` 16, `seed` 24, `draw`, and the blind's rows `blind_per_node`, `blind_through` 273, `arrival` and `wings`; the keys are the builder's, each folder's its own). `build_world.py` beside it reads the design alone and writes the world file, `--folder <folder>` elsewhere and the folder itself by default, with `--modes` calls the generator for the mode file (the two slits' builder always does), and writes `expectation.json` from the design by a formula, before any run and never from a run: a theorem of the law's lines (Bell's S = 478 / 169 by Lagrange's identity on equal parts, the GHZ's M = -4), an exact computation from them (the two slits' Huygens row per region, the bodies' rests and rotations at Gamma 6,000) or a sentence a run answers yes or no; the one number a blind may take from a file is the lay's count from the mode file (`laid`, the generator's reading of the laid record in quanta, no run's); the committed world, mode and expectation files are the builder's byte for byte, so a clean `git status` after the builder is the check that nothing was edited by hand. A blind row is one named entry under `blind` with four keys: `reading` (which lines of the output are read, over which window, by which tool or function), `blind` (the number or the sentence expected), `status` (where it comes from: a theorem, a computation from named lines, nothing read back from a run; where a line it rested on changed, the number as first written is kept beside its restatement under `as_first_written`, `recomputed` and `from`, the families round's folders) and `fence` (the side of the one line the row stands on: `clicks`, answered by the NodeDetectors' `click` and `parts` lines, the measurement, or `Lattice`, answered by a reading of the arrays labelled so, which is never a finding); a `status` and a `fence` also stand on the blind as a whole where its rows are prose (the bodies' looks, `verdict` and `label` "LATTICE"). Two blinds have a reader's format: the screen's (`verdict` "NODEDETECTOR", `node_detector`, `family`, `window`, `across`, `pattern`, `seed`, `counts` per region, `maxima`, `minima`, `central`, `visibility`, `quanta`, `laid`, `arrival`, `wings`, `aside`; `tools/click_counts.py`, section 5) and the gate's (`family`, `window`, `sides`, `settings`, `patterns`, `order`, `runs`, `combination` {`name`, `signs`}, `seed` and `blind` with `correlation`, `marginals`, `S` or `M` and the local credits, each with its `status` and `fence`; `tools/bell_gate.py`); a blind in neither format is read from the output's lines with the readers' functions (item 5). After `expectation.json` is written nothing edits it: a wrong blind stays wrong beside the run's number, and a changed line of the law restates the row with the first number kept.

4. **The run and its output.** At the tag v1.1.0, `PYTHONPATH=src python tools/run_inputs.py --out runs/two_slits examples/events/two_slits/two_slits.json` (several worlds by name, `--jobs 4` in parallel; never a glob over a folder, which takes the mode files, which the runner refuses) prints one line per world, `{"input": "two_slits.json", "verdict": "LAWFUL", "intervals": 130}`, a few seconds on one core, and writes `runs/two_slits/two_slits.output.json`, one JSON object: `input` (the world file's name), `verdict` (`LAWFUL`, or `REFUSED` with `reason`, the loader's, the guard's or the gate's refusal by name at load, or a refusal inside the run with its interval, the lines until then kept), `intervals` (the intervals run, the file's 130 here), `ended` (null here, the board grown to 269 columns of its largest 400 by the end; or the lawful end at a receding face grown to its largest size, {`interval`, `axis`, `side`, `largest`}, the run stopping rather than reflecting), `books` (per family of quanta `share`, `quanta`, `drift`, `pace` and `frozen`, a lattice diagnostic, section 5) and `lines`, every output line in the order written, each `event`, `label`, `interval`, `family`, `node_detector` and its own key: the `click` line (`label` "NODEDETECTOR", `inflow`), the net current into the region through the NodeDetector's front boundary Ports this interval, inward positive, in the current's units, and the `credit` line, the quantum drawn to a region, the one measurement (the two slits' twelve screen regions over the window [45, 130], N credits in all, section 5's numbers; the bare region `gap` none); the `parts` line (`label` "NODEDETECTOR", `levels`), per line of the record of several parts the signed sums [now, before] over the region at the interval's start (the GHZ's four per region; a world of one real line writes none); and the `density` line (`label` "LATTICE", `reading`, or `reading` null with `well` over a frozen Node), the family's density over a declared region where it changed, a lattice reading and no measurement; one line per interval, family and region where the number is not 0, and no line names a Node. What is a click: a `click` line, the NodeDetector's report, with the `parts` line beside it, the NodeDetector's read; what is a lattice reading: a `density` line, the `books`, and every number a tool reads from the arrays (`tools/body_rest.py`, `tools/look/record.py`), each labelled "LATTICE" and never a finding.

5. **The readers and the gates.** The reader the blind's format names, each a host tool holding no number of the law (section 5): `tools/click_counts.py` reads a screen (`examples/events/two_slits/`, `examples/events/which_way/`) by `PYTHONPATH=src python tools/click_counts.py --world <world>.json --output <world>.output.json --expectation <expectation>.json`; `tools/bell_gate.py` reads a gate's four worlds (`examples/events/bell/`, `examples/events/ghz/`) by `PYTHONPATH=src python tools/bell_gate.py --expectation <expectation>.json --outputs <the four output files>`; `tools/body_rest.py` reads the body's look (`examples/events/matter_alone/`), a lattice reading, absorption the worlds by name with `--expectation <expectation>.json` to print the blind beside the reading; every reader prints the blind's numbers beside its own and compares nothing. A blind in no reader's format (the which-way's `rows`; the trials' blinds of the Zeno, the anticoincidence and the resonance folders) is read with the readers' functions (`inflows` of `tools/click_counts.py` sums a region's `click` lines over the window) or by `tools/meeting_trials.py` and the folder's own reader, as its `blind_and_reading.md` says. The gates: `PYTHONPATH=src python tools/back_in_time.py --intervals 40 examples/events/two_slits/two_slits.json` prints `{"verdict": "MATCH", "intervals": 40, "interval": 1, "ended": null}` in a few seconds (section 6, step 5) (MISS names the first interval, array and Node that differ), and a reading is read only on MATCH; `python tools/check.py` before delivery selects the changed files and their consumers (a new folder's `build_world.py` for `ruff`, and the law's tests of `tools/every_pull_request.txt`, `tests/test_documents.py` and `tests/test_repository_language.py` among them, on every selection), `--dry-run` printing the selection and `--full` running the whole suite (section 8). The run's output under `runs/` is never committed.

6. **A worked example**, the two slits' folder as it is on `main`, in ten lines from the checkout's root, each with what it prints:

```bash
cat examples/events/two_slits/design.json        # 1. every number: the 45 x 48 board, the wall at x = 20 with its gaps y 17 to 19 and 29 to 31, the packet at k = pi / 4 from the column 10 at the amplitude 1241, the screen at x = 44 in twelve regions of four rows and the bare region gap, intervals 130, the window [45, 130], the seed 24, the blind's rows
cat examples/events/light.json                   # 2. the universe file: the four integers, the three holders (gravity, the charge, the binding holder) and matter's real line; the two slits' light is the holder of the sign's own record
PYTHONPATH=src python examples/events/two_slits/build_world.py     # 3. writes two_slits.json, two_slits.mode.json (by the generator, which this builder always calls) and the blind expectation.json, byte for byte the files there; git status stays clean
cat examples/events/two_slits/expectation.json   # 4. the blind, written before any run: the Huygens row per region, the pattern's maxima and minima, the laid count 1804 from the mode file, the arrival and the wings with their statuses
# 5. the generator left the tree 2026-10-24: the mode file beside the world is a frozen record and no input
PYTHONPATH=src python tools/run_inputs.py --out runs/two_slits examples/events/two_slits/two_slits.json      # 6. {"input": "two_slits.json", "verdict": "LAWFUL", "intervals": 130}, a few seconds
PYTHONPATH=src python tools/back_in_time.py --intervals 40 examples/events/two_slits/two_slits.json       # 7. {"verdict": "MATCH", "intervals": 40, "interval": 1, "ended": null}
# 6. the credit's reader left the tree 2026-10-24 with the generator
PYTHONPATH=src python tools/look/record.py examples/events/two_slits/two_slits.json --intervals 130 && PYTHONPATH=src python tools/look/page.py examples/events/two_slits/two_slits.look.json --blind examples/events/two_slits/expectation.json     # 9. the one page that shows it, a lattice reading with the NodeDetectors' bars beside the blind curve (section 6, step 10)
python tools/check.py                           # 10. the gates on the changed files before the pull request
```

The room of the loop's own module: `lattice.py` gains six lines for the swap at the top (the kept state, the two calls, the walls kept, the two readings), the act itself in `swap.py` after `shear.py`'s precedent, a module of its own. The tests' ratchet's room, given by name (the rule that the bulk must not come back): `tools/tests_shape.py` holds a `ROOM` table of the tests given room beyond the merge base, each with its lines and the reason, and the gate adds the room to the base's size while the named test stands with at most those lines; 4. **A body of several pieces stands in one part** (an engine note of 2026-10-17, confirmed on main's code; no code change; the code named left the tree 2026-10-24 with the declared bodies): `node_detector.books_of` sets a body's part as the argmax of its counts at the start, `act.written` re-sets it after every write, and the acts on transitions, `meeting.turned_labels`, `lists.absorption` and `lists.emission` (and, to 2026-10-20, the probe's and the conversion's), act on transitions out of `books.part` alone. So a body of N >= 2 pieces driven out of its ground part stops absorbing once an excited part holds the majority: on a plain copy of the engine, a body of three parts with N = 3 laid in the ground part and driven by a resonant holder stuck at [1, 0, 2], the minority part's transitions having no outcome.
